Actually $\pi_n = H_n(F_n)$ where $F_n$ fiber, computed via homology of effective complex $\mathrm{EC}(X_n)$ in that degree using `homology-groups` Smith.
Demo's $\pi7(P\infty R/P2R)$ and $\pi4(\Sigma K(A4,1))$ are this method (Xavier Dousson thesis).
Intake has only $\pi_1$ via 2-skeleton + $\pi_{*}(S^n)$ DB lookup; it cannot compute $\pi7$ of arbitrary finite complex — this functor $\pi_n: \mathsf{sSet}^{1\text{-conn},\text{eff}} \to \mathsf{Ab}$ is new.
Requires $H^{n+1}(-;\pi)$ model via Eilenberg-MacLane effective.

**7. Discrete vector fields** — Kenzo 1.1.8 upgrade (Forman) improves reductions for Eilenberg-Zilber and $K(\pi,n)$ effective homology by Morse reductions $C \to C^c$ via admissible vector field $V$ on cellular basis, with $f,g,h$ from $V$.
This is new discrete Morse category $\mathsf{DVF}(\mathsf{Ch})$ not in intake.

**8. Integration with derived functors** — Effective homology core is exactly derived tensor products: $C_*(X\times_\tau B) \simeq C_*(F) \otimes^L_{C_*(G)}$?
More precisely $C_*(F\times_\tau B) \cong C_*(F)\otimes_t C_*(B)$ with twisted differential = $C_*(F)\otimes^L C_*(B)$ perturbed, and cohomology operations $\operatorname{Ext}, \operatorname{Tor}$ over $ZZ$ via effective chain models feed homological algebra $\operatorname{Tor}, \operatorname{Ext}$ already noted but now with concrete topological $\otimes^L$.
Intake's Derived Hom/tensor — $\operatorname{Ext},\operatorname{Tor},D(R\text{-}\mathsf{Mod})$ with $R=ZZ$ and $\otimes^L$ as monoidal on $D(ZZ\text{-}\mathsf{Mod})$ is the algebraic owner; Kenzo's $\mathsf{EffHom}, \operatorname{Bar}, \operatorname{Cobar}, \mathrm{EZ}$ are topological adapters that $\otimes^L$ must consume — $\mathrm{EC} \otimes^L \mathrm{EC}'$ effective computes $\operatorname{Tor}$ over chain algebras.

**What does not generalize without new math**: General $X$ must be simply connected and with effective homology presented; non-nilpotent/unbounded homotopy requires additional spectral sequence convergence not implemented; real coefficients $R$ vs $ZZ$ Smith needs PID; $\pi_n(X)$ for non-finite $X$ (e.g. infinite CW) needs local effectiveness hypothesis to stay in $\mathsf{EffHom}$.

## Fundamental semantic language for Kenzo generalization — verbatim — 2026-09-15

For `EffHom / loop / Postnikov` to be statable, these must exist as categories/functors — not data $\operatorname{Moore}(Z/2,4)$:

**Base**

* $\mathsf{Sets}$, $NN$ — degrees $n : NN$, ranks $\operatorname{rank} \mathrm{EC}_n : NN$

* $\mathsf{sSet} : \mathsf{Cat}$ — simplicial set $X = (X_n, d_i, s_i)$ object; $\mathsf{sSet}_*$ pointed version $*\in X_0$; $\operatorname{Kan}(\mathsf{sSet})\subset \mathsf{sSet}$ subcategory where every horn $\Lambda^k[n]\to X$ has filler

* $\mathsf{sGrp} : \mathsf{Cat}$ — simplicial group $G$, underlying $G_n : \mathsf{Groups}$ with simplicial structure; forgetful $U: \mathsf{sGrp}\to \mathsf{sSet}$ via underlying Kan complex

* $\mathsf{Ch}(R) : \mathsf{AddCat}$ — chain complexes $C = (C_n, d_n: C_n\to C_{n-1})$ over $R=ZZ$ (more generally $R : \mathsf{CommRings}$), with shift $[1]$, cone $\operatorname{Cone}(f)$, tensor $C\otimes D$, internal hom

**Reductions / effective homology**

* $\operatorname{Red}(C,D) : \mathsf{Sets}$ — reduction $\rho=(f,g,h)$ as above, with axioms $fg=\mathrm{id}$, $gf+dh+hd=\mathrm{id}$, $fh=hg=hh=0$ (chain homotopy $h$); composition and transport

* $\operatorname{StrongEquiv}(C,\mathrm{EC})$ — strong equivalence $C \Leftarrow \hat{C} \Rightarrow \mathrm{EC}$ span of two reductions; object $\mathrm{EC} : \mathsf{Ch}^{\text{eff}}$ effective meaning $\mathrm{EC}_n = R^{\mathrm{rank}_n}$ free finite rank and $d_n$ given by finite matrix $\operatorname{Mat}_{\mathrm{rank}_{n-1}\times \mathrm{rank}_n}(R)$ computable

* $\mathsf{EffHom}(\mathsf{sSet})$ — objects $(X, \varepsilon_X)$ where $\varepsilon_X: C_*(X)\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{EC}_X$ strong equivalence; morphisms $f: X\to Y$ in $\mathsf{sSet}$ lift to $C_*(f): C_*(X)\to C_*(Y)$ compatible with $\varepsilon$; forgetful $U(X,\varepsilon)=X$, $\mathrm{EC}(X,\varepsilon)=\mathrm{EC}_X : \mathsf{Ch}^{\text{eff}}$.
  Interface is `X.effective_homology()` $\to (\mathrm{EC},\varepsilon)$ not free `effective_homology(X)`

* Perturbation $\delta: C\to C_{*-1}$ with $(d+\delta)^2=0$ small ($\mathrm{id}+\delta h$ invertible); Basic Perturbation Lemma $\operatorname{BPL}(\rho,\delta) \to \rho' : (C,d+\delta) \Leftrightarrow (D,d'+\delta')$ and Easy $\operatorname{EPL}$ — functors $\operatorname{PertRed} : \operatorname{Red}\times \operatorname{Pert} \to \operatorname{Red}$

**Simplicial / Eilenberg-Zilber**

* $\operatorname{Susp}(X), \operatorname{Cone}(f), \operatorname{DiskPasting}, \operatorname{CartProd}(X,Y)$ — constructions in $\mathsf{sSet}$; $C_*: \mathsf{sSet}\to \mathsf{Ch}$ normalized chain functor (Dold-Kan normalized)

* Eilenberg-Zilber $\mathrm{EZ}: C_*(X\times Y) \Leftrightarrow C_*(X)\otimes C_*(Y)$ reduction (Alexander-Whitney $f$, shuffle $g$, Shih $h$); twisted EZ $\operatorname{tEZ}(\tau): C_*(F\times_\tau B) \Leftrightarrow C_*(F)\otimes_t C_*(B)$ where $\otimes_t$ has twisted differential $\delta_\tau$ from twisting cochain $\tau: C_*(B)_{*+1}\to C_*(F)_*$ / twisting operator $\tau: B_{*+1}\to F_*$

* Bar $B A$ and cobar $\Omega C$ for dg algebra $A$ and dg coalgebra $C$: $B A = T^c(s\bar A)$ coalgebra, $\Omega C = T(s^{-1}\bar C)$ algebra — objects in $\mathsf{dgAlg}/\mathsf{dgCoalg}$; $C_*(G X) \simeq \Omega C_*(X)$ as $\mathsf{dgAlg}$ equivalence (Adams) underlying loop-space effective homology

**Fibrations**

* $\operatorname{Fibration} = (F, B, G, \tau, E=F\times_\tau B)$ where $G : \mathsf{sGrp}$ acts on $F$, $\tau: B\to G$ twisting operator satisfies $d0 \tau(b)=\tau(d0 b)\cdot\partial b$ etc.; projection $p: E\to B$ Kan fibration with fiber $F$; `E.total()` object of $\mathsf{sSet}$ with `efhm` from base+ fiber `efhm` via $\operatorname{tEZ}+\operatorname{BPL}$

* $\operatorname{LoopSpace}(X) = G X : \mathsf{sGrp}$ and $\operatorname{ClassifyingSpace}(G)=\bar{W}G : \mathsf{sSet}_*$ with $G \dashv \bar{W}$ adjunction $\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathsf{sGrp}}(G X, H) \cong \operatorname{Hom}_{\mathsf{sSet}_*}(X, \bar{W}H)$; iterates $\Omega^n X = G^n X$ as $\mathsf{sGrp}$/$\mathsf{sSet}$ with $\mathrm{EC}(\Omega^n X)$ via iterated $\Omega$ cobar + $\operatorname{BPL}$

**Eilenberg-MacLane / classifying**

* $K(\pi,n): \mathsf{AbGroups}\times NN \to \mathsf{sSet}^{\text{eff}}$ with $\pi_n(K(\pi,n))=\pi$, $\pi_{\neq n}=0$ and $\mathrm{EC}(K(\pi,n))$ effective via bar inductively $K(\pi,n)=B K(\pi,n-1)$ ($n>1$) or standard resolution ($K(Z,1)=S^1$ etc.); homology $H_*(K(\pi,n))$ via Smith on $\mathrm{EC}$

* $\operatorname{Smith} : \operatorname{Mat}_{m\times n}(ZZ) \to \operatorname{diag}(d1\mid d2\mid \ldots)$ invariant factors for $H_n(\mathrm{EC})$ — $H_n(\mathrm{EC})= ZZ^{\mathrm{rank}} \oplus \bigoplus_i ZZ/d_i$ from $d_n$ boundary matrices $\operatorname{Mat}(R)$ in $\mathsf{Ch}^{\text{eff}}$

**Postnikov / Whitehead tower**

* $\operatorname{Postnikov}(X): \mathsf{sSet}^{1\text{-conn}}\to \mathsf{Tower}$ where $X^{(n)}$ with $\pi_{>n}(X^{(n)})=0$, $\pi_{\leq n}=\pi_{\leq n}(X)$ and fibration $K(\pi_{n+1}, n+1)\hookrightarrow X^{(n+1)}\to X^{(n)}$ classified by k-invariant $\kappa_n\in H^{n+2}(X^{(n)};\pi_{n+1})$ as cohomology class $[c]\in H^{n+2}(\mathrm{EC}(X^{(n)}))$ represented by twisting cochain; dually Whitehead $X\langle n\rangle$  $(n-1)$-connected cover with same $\pi_{\geq n}$

* Functor $\pi_n: \mathsf{sSet}^{1\text{-conn},\text{eff}}\to \mathsf{Ab}$ via $\pi_n(X)=H_n(X\langle n\rangle)$ or $H_{n+1}(X^{(n+1)}, X)$; computable as `homology( EC(X<n>), n )` via Smith — Kenzo's `z2-whitehead`, `fibration-total`, `homology`

**Not fundamental**

* $\operatorname{Moore}(Z/2,4)$, $S^3\cup_2 D^3$, $P\infty R/P2R$, $\operatorname{rank} \mathrm{EC}5=23$, `shift`, $n=4$, `2 minutes/20 hours`, matrices in $C_*(X)$ — elements/morphisms in above

* $\varphi_\alpha: S^{n-1}\to K^{n-1}$ CW attaching (already intaken) is special case of $\mathsf{sSet}$ cell attachment $\Delta^n/\partial\Delta^n\to X$; effective homology reduces to that when $C_*(X)$ already effective

## Fundamental semantic language for stable/characteristic/chromatic machinery — verbatim — 2026-09-15

For Kenzo's $\pi_*$, Adams, BP to be statable without coding by fiat, these must exist as categories/functors — not numbers $\Sigma S^n=S^{n+1}$ or a bare `steenrod_square(n)` function:

**Homology theories**

* $\operatorname{HomologyTheory}: \mathsf{HoTop}_* \to \mathsf{GrAb}$ as functor satisfying Eilenberg-Steenrod axioms (homotopy, excision, exactness, dimension) for singular/simplicial models $H^{\text{sing}}_*(X;R)=H_*(C^{\text{sing}}_*(X)\otimes R)$ via $C^{\text{sing}}_*: \mathsf{sSet}\to \mathsf{Ch}(R)$ normalized / Moore complex, vs. extraordinary theories $E_*: \mathsf{HoTop}_*\to \mathsf{GrAb}$ (stable, wedge, suspension isomorphism $E_n(X)\cong E_{n+1}(\Sigma X)$ without dimension axiom); cohomology $E^*: \mathsf{HoTop}_*^{\text{op}}\to \mathsf{GrAb}$.
  Reduced vs. unreduced as $\tilde{E}_*(X)=\ker(E_*(X)\to E_*(\text{pt}))$.
  Object $E_*$ is the theory, not its value on a space.

**Moore, Eilenberg-MacLane, classifying**

* $\operatorname{Moore}(G,n): \mathsf{Ab}\to \mathsf{HoTop}_*$, $\tilde{H}_i(\operatorname{Moore}(G,n))=G$ if $i=n$ else $0$ (via $M(G,n)= \operatorname{cone}$ on Moore presentation $F1\to F0\to G$); $K(\pi,n): \mathsf{Groups}/\mathsf{Ab}\to \mathsf{HoTop}_*$ with $\pi_n=K(\pi,n)=\pi$ else $0$ and $\mathrm{EC}(K(\pi,n))$ effective as already intaken via $B^n K(\pi,0)$ bar iteration; $BG = B G = \bar{W}(N G)$ classifying space $G : \mathsf{Groups}\to \mathsf{HoTop}$ with universal bundle $EG\to BG$ $EG$ contractible and $\Omega BG \simeq G$ as $H$-group, functor $B: \mathsf{sGrp}\to \mathsf{sSet}_*$ already in Kenzo layer, now as $B: \operatorname{Grp}(\mathsf{Top})\to \mathsf{HoTop}$.
  Distinguish $K(\pi,n)=B^n \pi$.

**Model categories**

* $\mathsf{ModelCat} : \mathsf{Cat}$ with classes $W$ (weak equivalences, 2-of-3), $\mathrm{Cof}$, $\mathrm{Fib}$ satisfying lifting $\mathrm{Cof} \pitchfork \mathrm{Fib}\cap W$, $\mathrm{Cof}\cap W \pitchfork \mathrm{Fib}$ and factorization $f = p\circ i$ with $i\in \mathrm{Cof}\cap W, p\in \mathrm{Fib}$ etc. Examples: $\mathsf{sSet}$ (Kan-Quillen: $W$=weak homotopy equivalences on realization, $\mathrm{Cof}$=monomorphisms, $\mathrm{Fib}$=Kan fibrations), $\mathsf{Top}$ (Serre/Quillen), $\mathsf{Ch}(R)$ (projective: $W$=quasi-isomorphisms $\mathrm{qis}$, $\mathrm{Cof}$=degreewise split monos with projective cokernel, $\mathrm{Fib}$=degreewise epis).
  Homotopy category $\operatorname{Ho}(M)=M[W^{-1}]$ localization as $\infty$-categorical localization; derived category $D(R)=\operatorname{Ho}(\mathsf{Ch}(R))$.
  Quillen adjunction $F: M\rightleftarrows N:U$ with $F(\mathrm{Cof})\subset \mathrm{Cof}, U(\mathrm{Fib})\subset \mathrm{Fib}$ and derived adjunction $LF \dashv RU$ on $\operatorname{Ho}$.

**Derived / homotopy categories already intaken but made explicit**

* $D(R\text{-}\mathsf{Mod})=\mathsf{Ch}(R\text{-}\mathsf{Mod})[\mathrm{qis}^{-1}]$ and $D(R)=\operatorname{Ho}(\mathsf{Ch}(R))$ triangulated (shift $[1]$, cones, distinguished triangles $X\to Y\to \operatorname{Cone}\to X[1]$); $\mathsf{HoTop}_* = \mathsf{Top}_*[\mathrm{WHE}^{-1}]$, $\operatorname{Ho}(\mathsf{sSet})=\mathsf{sSet}[W^{-1}]$ via $|-| \dashv \operatorname{Sing}$.
  Interface $L f^*, R f_*, \otimes^L, R\operatorname{Hom}$ already intaken in derived $\operatorname{Ext}/\operatorname{Tor}$ lead — now as left/right derived of Quillen functors on model categories, not ad-hoc free resolutions.

**Loop / suspension / smash (pointed)**

* $\Sigma: \mathsf{HoTop}_*\to \mathsf{HoTop}_*$ via $\Sigma X = S^1\wedge X = (I\wedge X)/(0\wedge X \cup 1\wedge X)$ as homotopy pushout $*\leftarrow X\to CX$ (cone), $\Omega: \mathsf{HoTop}_*\to \mathsf{HoTop}_*$ via $\Omega X = \operatorname{Map}_*(S^1,X)$ as homotopy pullback $*\to X\leftarrow X^I$ (path space).
  Adjunction $\Sigma \dashv \Omega$ as homotopy adjunction $[\Sigma X,Y]\cong[X,\Omega Y]$ graded.
  Smash $\wedge: \mathsf{HoTop}_*\times \mathsf{HoTop}_*\to \mathsf{HoTop}_*$ monoidal with unit $S^0$, associator, braiding; internal hom $\operatorname{Map}_*$.
  Computable is not fiat $\Sigma S^n = S^{n+1}$ — must exhibit $\mathsf{sSet}$ model $\Sigma\Delta^n/\partial\Delta^n \cong S^{n+1}$ via simplicial suspension $\Sigma X = (X\times \Delta^1)/(X\times \partial\Delta^1 \cup *\times \Delta^1)$ with $\mathrm{EC}(\Sigma X)$ via $\mathrm{EC}(X)$ shift $C_*(\Sigma X)\cong C_*(X)[1]$ effective, not a table entry.

* $\mathsf{sSet}_*$ models: $\Sigma = S^1\wedge -$, $\Omega = G \dashv \bar{W}$ Kan loop group equivalence already in Kenzo section, now as pointed simplicial $\Sigma \dashv \Omega$ Quillen.

**Bar / cobar**

* Bar $B: \mathsf{dgAlg}\to \mathsf{dgCoalg}$ $B A = T^c(s\bar A)$ with coproduct deconcatenation and differential $d_B = d_A + \text{product}$, Cobar $\Omega: \mathsf{dgCoalg}\to \mathsf{dgAlg}$ $\Omega C = T(s^{-1}\bar C)$ with differential $d_\Omega = d_C + \text{coproduct}$; adjunction $\Omega \dashv B$ and $C_*(G X) \simeq \Omega C_*(X)$ (Adams) and $C_*(B G) \simeq B C_*(G)$, both as $\mathsf{EffHom}$ reductions perturbed via BPL — loop effective homology 4 already, now as $\operatorname{Ho}(\mathsf{dgAlg})\simeq \operatorname{Ho}(\mathsf{dgCoalg})$ equivalence behind spectra.

**Towers**

* $\operatorname{PostnikovTower}(X) = (X^{(n)}, p_n: X^{(n)}\to X^{(n-1)})$ inverse tower with $X^{(n)}$ $n$-truncated ($\pi_{>n}=0$, $\pi_{\leq n}=\pi_{\leq n}(X)$), fiber $K(\pi_{n+1}, n+1)$ classified by k-invariant $\kappa_n\in H^{n+2}(X^{(n)};\pi_{n+1})$; $X \simeq \operatorname{holim} X^{(n)}$.
  Dually $\operatorname{WhiteheadTower}(X)$ $\cdots\to X\langle n+1\rangle\to X\langle n\rangle\to\cdots\to X$ with $X\langle n\rangle$ $(n-1)$-connected cover, $\pi_{\geq n}=\pi_{\geq n}(X)$, $\pi_{<n}=0$.
  More generally infinite fibration tower $\cdots\to E_{n+1}\to E_n\to\cdots$ and cofibration tower $X_0\to X_1\to\cdots$ as objects $\operatorname{Tower}(\mathrm{Fib})=\operatorname{Fun}(N^{\text{op}}, M)$ / $\operatorname{Tower}(\mathrm{Cof})=\operatorname{Fun}(N,M)$ with $\operatorname{holim}/\operatorname{hocolim}$.
  Killing homotopy $X\to X'$ with $\pi_{>n}(X')=\pi_{>n}(X)$, $\pi_{\leq n}=0$ via cell attachment $X' = X \cup_{\text{representative of } \pi_n} D^{n+1}$ iteratively.

**Homotopy fibers/cofibers, skeleta, maps**

* `Skeleta` $\operatorname{sk}_n X$, $\operatorname{cosk}_n X$ in $\mathsf{sSet}/\mathsf{CW}$ as $n$-truncation via $\Delta_{\leq n}$ left Kan extension; $\mathsf{CW}_n \subset \mathsf{CW}_{n+1}$ via pushout $\coprod_\alpha S^{n-1}\to K^{n-1}$ already intaken, now with $\operatorname{CWMaps}: \mathsf{CW}\to \mathsf{CW}$ whose cellular approximation is functor.
  `SimplicialMaps` similarly.
  Homotopy fiber $\operatorname{hofib}(f)= X\times^h_Y * = X\times_Y Y^I$ and cofiber $\operatorname{hocofib}(f)= Y \cup^h_X * = Y \cup_X X\wedge I$; fiber sequence $F\to E\to B$ vs cofiber $X\to Y\to C_f$ as distinguished triangles in $\operatorname{Ho}(M)$.

**Graded structures**

* $\mathsf{GrAb}_{ZZ^n}, \mathsf{GrRings}, \mathsf{GrMod}, \mathsf{GrAlg}$ with bigraded $E^{p,q}_r$, $\pi_{p+q}(X)$ bigraded, $\operatorname{Ext}^{p,q}, \operatorname{Tor}_{p,q}$ as usual.
  More generally $G\text{-}\mathsf{GrMod}$ for $G: \mathsf{AbGroups}$ (e.g. $ZZ$, $ZZ/2$, $ZZ^n$, $\mathrm{RO}(G)$) as functor $\operatorname{Gr}: \mathsf{Ab}\to \mathsf{Cat}$ $M=\bigoplus_{g\in G} M_g$ with $G$-graded tensor $M\otimes N$ in degree $g+h$.
  Needed for $E_2^{p,q}=H^p(B;H^q(F))$ etc.

**$\pi_*$ as graded Lie**

* $\pi_*(X)=\bigoplus_{n\geq 1}\pi_n(X)$ as graded Lie algebra under Whitehead product $[\alpha,\beta]\in\pi_{p+q-1}$ with Jacobi and graded antisymmetry, and Toda brackets $\langle\alpha,\beta,\gamma\rangle\subset\pi_{p+q+r+1}$ secondary operation where $\alpha\beta=0, \beta\gamma=0$ as coset of indeterminacy; higher Toda operations.
  Structure maps $[-,-]: \pi_p\otimes\pi_q\to\pi_{p+q-1}$ as $\mathsf{HoTop}_*(S^p\wedge S^q\to S^{p+q-1})$ via universal Whitehead.

**Sullivan minimal models**

* $\mathsf{DGA}_{Q}^{\geq 0}$ Sullivan algebras $(\Lambda V,d)$ with $V=\bigoplus_{n\geq 1}V^n$ graded vector space, $\Lambda$ free graded-commutative, $d$ decomposable and nilpotent filtration; minimal $d(V)\subset\Lambda^{\geq 2}V$ and $V$ well-ordered so $d(v_i)\in\Lambda(V_{<i})$.
  Functor $A_{\mathrm{PL}}: \mathsf{sSet}\to \mathsf{DGA}_Q$ (PL forms) and minimal model $M_X \to A_{\mathrm{PL}}(X)$ quasi-isomorphism with $M_X = (\Lambda V,d)$ minimal and $V^n \cong \operatorname{Hom}(\pi_n(X),Q)$ for nilpotent finite-type $X$; model category $\mathsf{DGA}_Q$ with $W=\mathrm{qis}$.
  Not a free `minimal_model(X)` returning matrices — object of $\operatorname{Ho}(\mathsf{DGA})$ with $\pi_*\otimes Q$ read off $V$.

**Stabilization / spectra**

* $\operatorname{Stab}(C)$ stabilization of pointed $\infty$-category $C$ with finite limits/colimits as $\mathsf{Sp}(C)=\lim(\cdots\to C \to\Omega C \to\Omega C)$ or $\operatorname{Exc}_*(S^{\text{fin}}_*, C)$ excisive functors; universal property $\operatorname{Fun}^{\text{lex}}(\mathsf{Sp}(C),D)\simeq\operatorname{Fun}^{\text{lex}}(C,D)$ stable.
  For $C=\mathsf{Top}_*$, $\mathsf{Sp} = \mathsf{Sp}(\mathsf{Top}_*)$ is category $\mathsf{Spectra}$ stable, with $\Sigma^\infty: \mathsf{Top}_* \rightleftarrows \mathsf{Sp}: \Omega^\infty$ stabilization adjunction $\Sigma^\infty \dashv \Omega^\infty$ and $\Sigma^\infty X = (\Sigma^n X)_n$ sequential spectrum.
  Models: sequential spectra $E=(E_n, \sigma_n: \Sigma E_n\to E_{n+1})$ vs $\Omega$-spectra $\sigma^\flat: E_n\to\Omega E_{n+1}$ weak equivalence, symmetric spectra with $\Sigma_n$ action.
  Sphere spectrum $S = \Sigma^\infty S^0$, $S^n = \Sigma^\infty S^n$ as $S$ shift $S[n]$.
  Suspension spectrum $\Sigma^\infty_+: \mathsf{Top}\to\mathsf{Sp}$ as $\Sigma^\infty(X_+)$ unreduced ($X_+=X\sqcup *$), left adjoint to $\Omega^\infty$ on unpointed.
  Smash $\wedge: \mathsf{Sp}\times\mathsf{Sp}\to\mathsf{Sp}$ symmetric monoidal with unit $S$, internal hom $F(-,-)$.

* $\operatorname{Ho}(\mathsf{Spectra})$ triangulated with shift $\Sigma = S^1\wedge -$.

**$E_\infty$ / ring spectra, modules, mod $p$ reduction**

* $\operatorname{CAlg}(\mathsf{Sp})=E_\infty\text{-}\operatorname{Alg}$ as commutative algebra objects in $\mathsf{Sp}$ via $E_\infty$ operad $\operatorname{Comm}_{E_\infty}$; $\operatorname{Mod}_R$ for $R: E_\infty$ as $\mathsf{Sp}^{R\text{-mod}}$ stable symmetric monoidal $\otimes_R$.
  Examples: $S, HZ, HF_p, \mathrm{MU}, \mathrm{MO}, \mathrm{MSO}, K, \mathrm{BP}, E_n$.
  Mod $p$ reduction: at module level first $M/p = M\otimes^L_Z F_p : D(Z\text{-mod})$ via two-term $Z\to^p Z$, then $A/p = A\otimes^L_{E_\infty} HF_p : \operatorname{CAlg}_{HF_p}$ for $A: \operatorname{CAlg}_{HZ}$ or $A: \operatorname{CAlg}_S$; more generally $R$-algebra $A$ at prime $\mathfrak{p}\in\operatorname{Spec} R$ via $\kappa(\mathfrak{p})=\operatorname{Frac}(R/\mathfrak{p})$ or $R_\mathfrak{p}$ completion $A_{\mathfrak{p}}=A\otimes^L_R R_\mathfrak{p}$ and cofiber $A\to A_{\mathfrak{p}}\to A/\mathfrak{p}^\infty$.
  Need $\otimes^L$ already intaken as $D(R\text{-}\mathsf{Mod})$ monoidal.

**Steenrod / Adams / characteristic**

* Steenrod algebra $A = A_2, A_p : \mathsf{GrAlg}_{F_p}$ with $A = \operatorname{End}_{\mathsf{Sp}}(HF_p\otimes HF_p) \cong H^*(HF_p;F_p)$ and basis $\mathrm{Sq}^i$ ($p=2$) / $P^i, \beta$ ($p>2$) with Adem relations and Cartan $\mathrm{Sq}^k(xy)=\sum_{i+j=k}\mathrm{Sq}^i(x)\mathrm{Sq}^j(y)$; action $A \otimes H^*(X;F_p)\to H^*(X;F_p)$ as `Dyer-Lashof` unstable module over $A$.
  $\operatorname{CohomologyOperations}: \mathsf{HoTop}^{\text{op}}\to \mathsf{GrSets}$ $H^n(-;F_p)\to H^{n+k}(-;F_p)$ represented by $K(F_p,n)$.

* `Adams spectral sequence` $E_2^{s,t}=\operatorname{Ext}^{s,t}_{A}(H^*(X),F_p) \Rightarrow \pi_{t-s}(X)^{\wedge}_p$ (stable for $X: \mathsf{Sp}$, unstable via $\operatorname{Ext}$ over Lambda algebra); `Adams resolution` $X\to I^\bullet$ with $I_s = HF_p\wedge\bar{HF_p}^{\wedge s}\wedge X$ tower $\cdots\to X_{s+1}\to X_s$ whose $E_1 = \pi_*(I_s/I_{s+1})$.
  Next layers: `Adams-Novikov SS` $E_2^{s,t}=\operatorname{Ext}^{s,t}_{\mathrm{MU}_*\mathrm{MU}}(\mathrm{MU}_*,\mathrm{MU}_*(X))\Rightarrow\pi_{t-s}(X)$ with $\mathrm{MU}_*\mathrm{MU}$ Hopf algebroid, $\mathrm{BP}$ Brown-Peterson $\mathrm{BP}_*=Z_{(p)}[v1,\ldots]$ ($|v_n|=2(p^n-1)$) as summand of $\mathrm{MU}_{(p)}$ via Quillen idempotent, $\mathrm{BP}_*\mathrm{BP}$ and `Adams-Novikov` for $\mathrm{BP}$.
  Division algebras over $Q$ via Brauer $\operatorname{Br}(k)=H^2(\operatorname{Gal}, \bar k^\times)$, central simple.

**Cobordism / characteristic classes**

* `Cobordism rings` $\mathrm{MO}_* = \pi_*(\mathrm{MO})$ unoriented ($\mathrm{MO} = \operatorname{Thom}(O)$ with $\pi_*(\mathrm{MO})=F_2[x_i]$), $\mathrm{MU}_*$ complex ($\mathrm{MU} = \operatorname{Thom}(U)$, $\pi_*(\mathrm{MU})=Z[x_1,x_2,\ldots]$ $|x_i|=2i$, Lazard), $\mathrm{MSO}_*$, $\mathrm{MSpin}$, $\mathrm{MSp}$.
  Thom spectrum $M(G)=\operatorname{Thom}(EG\times_G R^n)$ and Pontryagin-Thom $\Omega^{\text{fr}}_* = \pi_*(S)$, $\Omega^{\text{un}}_*=\pi_*(\mathrm{MO})$ etc.

* `Characteristic classes` as natural transformations $H^*(BO)\to H^*(-)$ etc.: Stiefel-Whitney $w_i\in H^i(BO;F_2)$ via $BO = \operatorname{colim} \operatorname{Gr}(k,\infty)$, $w = \sum w_i = \prod(1+x_i)$ total, Whitney sum $w(\xi\oplus\eta)=w(\xi)\cup w(\eta)$, Pontryagin $p_i\in H^{4i}(BSO;Z[1/2])$ via Chern $p_i(\xi)=(-1)^i c_{2i}(\xi\otimes C)$, Chern $c_i\in H^{2i}(BU;Z)$, Euler $e\in H^n(BSO(n))$.
  Numbers $\langle\prod w_{i_k}^{e_k} \cup \mu_X, [X]\rangle\in F_2$ and $\langle\prod p_j^{e_j}, [X]\rangle\in Z$ as $\operatorname{CharNumbers}: \mathsf{Bordism}\to F_2/Z$ detecting bordism class (Thom).
  More general $E^*(BG)$ characteristic maps for $G=T, U(n), O(n), \text{etc.}$ via $H^*(BG;R)$ / $E^*(BG)$ as `Cohomology of classifying`.

**What else needs general + bigraded**

* $E\infty$ pages $E_r^{p,q}$ as objects of $\mathsf{GrMod}_{ZZ^2}(R)$ already intaken bigraded, now with differentials $d_r: E_r^{p,q}\to E_r^{p+r, q-r+1}$ and convergence $E_\infty^{p,q}=F^pH^{p+q}/F^{p+1}$ for filtered $H^*$.
  Used in Serre, Atiyah-Hirzebruch $E_2^{p,q}=H^p(X; E^q(\text{pt}))\Rightarrow E^{p+q}(X)$, Adams $E_2^{s,t}$, Adams-Novikov $E_2^{s,t}$.

**Not fundamental**

* Specific $\mathrm{Sq}^1(x)=\beta x$, $\mathrm{MU}_*=Z[x1,..]$ generator $x1=CP^1$, $\pi_*(S)=Z/24$ etc. — elements $x_i: \pi_{2i}(\mathrm{MU})$ and classes $w_i: H^i(BO)$ as morphisms $BO\to K(F_2,i)$, not new categories

* $\Sigma S^n\simeq S^{n+1}$ must be proven as $|\Sigma\Delta^n/\partial|\cong S^{n+1}$ via $\mathrm{EC}$ shift, not table — instance of $\Sigma \dashv \Omega$ on $\mathsf{sSet}$

## Desired capability: Eilenberg-Moore sseq operationalized for effective computations — note 2026-09-15

Note the Eilenberg-Moore spectral sequence should be operationalized for effective computations — not a table of $E_2=E_\infty$ coincidences.

* EM is not a formal $E_2^{p,q}=\operatorname{Tor}$ display.
  For a (homotopy) pullback $E = X \times_B Y$ of spaces over $B$ with $B$ 1-connected (more generally path-connected, nilpotent), and for a fibration $F \hookrightarrow E \to B$ as $E = F \times_B *$, the Eilenberg-Moore sseq is the spectral sequence of the two-sided bar construction $B(C_*(X), C_*(\Omega B), C_*(Y))$:

  $$
  \begin{aligned}
  &E_2^{p,q} = \operatorname{Tor}^{H_*(\Omega B)}_{p,q}(H_*(X), H_*(Y)) \Rightarrow H_{p+q}(X \times_B Y) \\
  &\operatorname{Cotor}^{H_*(B)}_{p,q}(H_*(X), H_*(Y)) \Rightarrow H_{p+q}(X \times_B Y) \quad \text{(coalgebra form when } B \text{ coaugmented)}
  \end{aligned}
  $$

  and cohomology $E_2^{p,q}=\operatorname{Ext}^{p,q}_{H^*(B)}(H^*(X),H^*(Y)) \Rightarrow H^{p+q}(X\times_B Y)$ / $E_2^{p,q}=\operatorname{Ext}_{H_*(\Omega B)}^{p,q}$ dual.
  Specialization to a fibration is $Y=*$.
  Without derived $\operatorname{Tor}$ over $H_*(\Omega B)$ / $\operatorname{Cotor}$ over $H_*(B)$ and bar/cobar, this is not statable.

* Operationalized means: from $B,X,Y : \mathsf{sSet}$ with $B$ effective (hence $C_*(B)$ and $H_*(\Omega B)$ via cobar $\Omega C_*(B)$), construct $B(C_*(X), C_*(\Omega B), C_*(Y))$ as effective chain complex via reductions, and compute $E_r^{p,q}$ as objects of $\mathsf{GrMod}_{ZZ^2}(R)$ ($R=ZZ, F_p$ via `smith` base-change) with explicit differentials $d_r: E_r^{p,q}\to E_r^{p+r, q-r+1}$ induced by bar filtration $F^pB = B^{\leq p}$ and perturbation $\delta_\tau$.
  Interface must be `(f: X->B, g: Y->B).eilenberg_moore(r) -> E_r` and `E_r.differential(p,q)` and `E_r.next_page()` and convergence $E_\infty^{p,q}=F^p H_{p+q}/F^{p+1}$ as filtration quotient of $H_*(X\times_B Y)$ via $\mathrm{EC}$ of pullback $X\times_B Y = X\times_\tau (\Omega B) \times Y$ twisted product, not a bare list of groups.

* Effective computation requires: loop space effective homology $C_*(\Omega B) \simeq \Omega C_*(B)$ already intaken (Kenzo $\Omega$ cobar + BPL), bar construction $B(A,M,N)=M\otimes T(s\bar A)\otimes N$ with differential $d_B = d_M\otimes 1\otimes 1 +1\otimes d_A\otimes 1+1\otimes 1\otimes d_N + \text{twisting from product/coproduct}$, two-sided bar $B(R, C_*(\Omega B), H_*(X))$ etc., and the reduction $B(C_*(X),C_*(\Omega B),C_*(Y)) \Leftrightarrow B(H_*(X),H_*(\Omega B),H_*(Y))$ via Eilenberg-Moore comparison when $B$ 1-connected; differentials must be computable from $\mathrm{EC}$ boundary matrices via `smith` over $ZZ$ or over $F_p$ after $\otimes^L F_p$.

* In easy cases when $H_*(B)$ is free / $B$ formal and $H_*(\Omega B)$ known, $E_2$ already gives answer — but general case requires $d_r$ for $r\geq 2$ and must not declare $E_2=E_\infty$ by fiat.
  For concrete fibrations $K(Z/2,1)\hookrightarrow E\to S^2$, $\Omega S^n\to PS^n\to S^n$ (path-loop), Postnikov fibrations $K(\pi,n+1)\hookrightarrow X^{(n+1)}\to X^{(n)}$, the EM $E_2$ via $\operatorname{Tor}$ must be returned as effective $\mathsf{GrMod}$ and $E_3, E_\infty$ via actual $d_2$ from twisting cochain / Massey products, certifying the filtration on $H_*(E)$ vs. direct $\mathrm{EC}(E)$ homology (agreement test).

* Requires: derived tensor already intaken $\otimes^L : D(R\text{-}\mathsf{Mod})\times D(R\text{-}\mathsf{Mod})\to D(R\text{-}\mathsf{Mod})$ with $\operatorname{Tor}_{p,q}=H_{p+q}( -\otimes^L -)$ in graded context; $C_*(\Omega B)$ as dg algebra ($H_*(\Omega B)=\operatorname{Ext}_{C_*(B)}(R,R)$) via cobar; $B$ as Reedy / model fibrant $B$ so $X\times_B Y$ is $h\times$; effective homology $\varepsilon_B, \varepsilon_X, \varepsilon_Y$ for base/fiber/pullback.
  Without model category fibrancy $F \hookrightarrow E\to B$ (`Kan fibration` in $\mathsf{sSet}$, Serre in $\mathsf{Top}$) the pullback is not homotopy pullback and EM does not apply.

* Relation to prior leads: EM $E_2=\operatorname{Tor}$ is not a new $\operatorname{Tor}$; it is the topological $\operatorname{Tor}^{H_*(\Omega B)}$ instance of the same derived $\operatorname{Ext}/\operatorname{Tor}/D(R\text{-}\mathsf{Mod})$ lead ($R=ZZ$) with $\otimes^L_{H_*(\Omega B)}$.
  Bar/cobar lead already provides $B/\Omega$.
  Serre sseq lead already provides $E_r$ interface; EM reuses that `SpectralSequence` object with $E_2^{p,q}=\operatorname{Tor}$ instead of $E_2^{p,q}=H^p(B;H^q(F))$, and with bar filtration instead of skeletal filtration.
  Do not build a second `SpectralSequenceEM` type duplicating $E_r$.

Intended owners: `categories/homotopy/eilenberg_moore.py` (`EilenbergMooreSpectralSequence` with $E_2 = \operatorname{Tor}^{H_*(\Omega B)}(H_*(X),H_*(Y))$ / $\operatorname{Cotor}^{H_*(B)}$ via `Bar`, `E_r.next_page()`, convergence to $H_*(X\times_B Y)$), delegating to `categories/topology/effective_homology.py` (`BPL` for $B(A,M,N)$), `categories/derived/tensor_product.py` ($\operatorname{Tor}$ as $\otimes^L$), `categories/algebras/bar_cobar.py` (`Bar`, `Cobar`, twisting cochain $\tau$), `categories/homotopy/model_categories.py` (`Kan fibration`, `h-pullback`). Not a free `eilenberg_moore(X,Y,B)` returning lists — `(X->B<-Y).eilenberg_moore()` on the cospan in $\operatorname{Ho}(\mathsf{sSet})$ with $E_r^{p,q}$ as $\mathsf{GrMod}_{ZZ^2}$ objects and differentials as morphisms, converging to `EC(X x_B Y).homology()`.

## Desired capability: hypercohomology and Čech cohomology operationalized and effective — note 2026-09-15

Also note that hypercohomology and Čech cohomology should similarly be operationalized and effective — not formal $H^i(X,F)$ symbols or a bare `cech_cohomology(cover)` list.

* Hypercohomology is not $H^i(X,F)$ for a single sheaf.
  For a complex of sheaves $F^\bullet \in \mathsf{Ch}(\mathsf{Sh}(X))$ ($\mathsf{Sh}(X)=\mathsf{Sh}(X_{\text{ét}}), \mathsf{QCoh}, \mathsf{Ab}, \text{etc.}$) on a site $X$ with global sections $\Gamma(X,-): \mathsf{Sh}(X)\to \mathsf{Ab}$ and derived $R\Gamma: D(\mathsf{Sh}(X))\to D(\mathsf{Ab})$, hypercohomology is $H^i(X, F^\bullet) = H^i(R\Gamma(F^\bullet)) = R^i\Gamma(F^\bullet) : \mathsf{Ab}$ with $R\Gamma$ the total right derived of $\Gamma$ via $K$-injective resolutions $F^\bullet \to I^\bullet$ (Spaltenstein) and $R\Gamma(F^\bullet)=\Gamma(I^\bullet)$ as effective complex object $R\Gamma(F^\bullet) : \mathsf{Ch}(\mathsf{Ab})$ / $D(\mathsf{Ab})$.
  Specialization to a single sheaf is $F[0]$; general $F^\bullet$ is needed for $R f_*(\Omega^\bullet_{X/S})$ (Gauss-Manin) $H^k_{\text{dR}}=R^k f_*\Omega^\bullet$, $R f_*(Q_\ell)$ for constructible $\ell$-adic, Hodge $R\Gamma(X, \Omega^p)$, de Rham $R\Gamma_{\text{dR}}=R\Gamma(X, \Omega^\bullet_X)$, etc. Without $R\Gamma$ as $D$, hypercohomology is not statable.

* Čech cohomology is the simplicial cover method that computes $R\Gamma$ effectively.
  For a covering $U = \{U_i \to X\}_{i\in I}$ in site $(C_X,J)$ (e.g. étale covering family, Zariski open cover, $\mathsf{sSet}$ hypercover), form Čech nerve $N(U)_p = \coprod_{i_0<\ldots<i_p} U_{i_0\ldots i_p}$ as simplicial object in $C_X$ with $U_{i_0\ldots i_p}=U_{i_0}\times_X \ldots\times_X U_{i_p}$ fiber product.
  For $F \in \mathsf{Sh}(X)$ define Čech cochains $\check{C}^p(U,F)=\prod F(U_{i_0\ldots i_p})$ with Čech differential $d_{\check{C}}: \check{C}^p\to \check{C}^{p+1}$ alternating sum of restriction maps $\mathrm{res}_{k}: F(U_{i_0\ldots\hat{k}\ldots}) \to F(U_{i_0\ldots})$.
  Then $\check{C}^\bullet(U,F) : \mathsf{Ch}(\mathsf{Ab})$ and $\check{H}^p(U,F)=H^p(\check{C}^\bullet)$.
  For complex $F^\bullet$, double $\check{C}^{p,q}=\check{C}^p(U,F^q)$ with $d_{\check{C}} + (-1)^p d_{F}$ and total $\operatorname{Tot}^\bullet(U,F^\bullet)$; hyper-Čech is $\check{H}^p(U,F^\bullet)=H^p(\operatorname{Tot})$.
  This is the $U$-effective model of $R\Gamma(F^\bullet)$ when $U$ is $F^\bullet$-acyclic (Leray: $H^q(U_{i_0\ldots}, F^r)=0$ for $q>0$, or $U_{i_0\ldots}$ affine for $\mathsf{QCoh}$).

* Operationalized and effective means: given $X : \mathsf{Sites}$ + $F^\bullet : \mathsf{Ch}(\mathsf{Sh}(X))$ (with $F^q$ presented as $\mathsf{Sh}$ objects on site) + covering $U : \operatorname{CoveringFamily}(X)$ with $U_{i_0\ldots}$ effectively computable (e.g. affine opens $\operatorname{Spec} R_{i_0\ldots}$, étale $\operatorname{Spec} S \to X$), construct $\check{C}^\bullet(U,F)$ / $\operatorname{Tot}^\bullet$ as effective chain complex $\mathrm{EC}(U,F^\bullet) : \mathsf{Ch}^{\text{eff}}$ with finite-rank $\mathrm{EC}_n$ in each degree and explicit matrices $d_n: \operatorname{Mat}$, via reductions from effective homology of the local sections $F(U_{i_0\ldots})$.
  Interface must be $F^\bullet$`.cech_complex(U)` $\to \check{C}^\bullet$, $F^\bullet$`.cech_cohomology(U,p)` $\to \check{H}^p$, and $R\Gamma(F^\bullet)$ as $D$ object with Čech-to-derived spectral sequence $E_1^{p,q}=\check{H}^q$?
  Actually $E_1^{p,q}=\check{C}^p(U, H^q(F^\bullet))$ no — for hypercohomology $E_1^{p,q}=\check{H}^q$?
  Standard: $E_1^{p,q}= \check{C}^p(U, H^q(F^\bullet)) \Rightarrow H^{p+q}(X,F^\bullet)$ vs. $E_2^{p,q}=H^p(X, H^q(F^\bullet)) \Rightarrow H^{p+q}$ — the Čech-to-derived sseq $E_2^{p,q}=\check{H}^p(U, H^q(F^\bullet)) \Rightarrow H^{p+q}(X,F^\bullet)$.
  Must expose $E_r^{p,q} : \mathsf{GrMod}$ with $d_r$ and convergence $E_\infty^{p,q}=F^pH^{p+q}/F^{p+1}$, and test acyclicity: when $U$ is $F^\bullet$-acyclic, comparison $\check{H}^p(U,F^\bullet) \cong H^p(X,F^\bullet)$ as isomorphism $\operatorname{Tot}(U,F^\bullet) \Leftrightarrow R\Gamma(F^\bullet)$ via Čech augmentation $F^\bullet \to \check{C}^\bullet$ quasi-isomorphism.

* Effective weakens finiteness: $\check{C}^p$ need not be finite — $\prod_{i_0<\ldots}$ over infinite cover is not effective.
  Effective hypothesis is $U$ finite cover (Zariski finite affine cover, finite étale cover, finite hypercover) and each $F(U_{i_0\ldots})$ effective (e.g. $\mathsf{QCoh}$ on affine $\operatorname{Spec} R$ has $\Gamma(U_{i_0\ldots},F)=M_{i_0\ldots} : R\text{-mod}$ computable, $\mathsf{Sh}$ on finite simplicial nerve).
  Then $\operatorname{Tot}$ is finite double complex and $\mathrm{EC}(\operatorname{Tot})$ via $\mathrm{EC}$ of each $M_{i_0\ldots}$ + perturbation from nerve differentials $d_{\check{C}}$ as `BPL` twist on tensor with simplicial cochain $C_*(N(U))$.

* In easy effective cases when cover is finite affine and $F^q$ is $\mathsf{QCoh}$ vector bundle / $Q_\ell$ lisse with $H^{>0}=0$ on affines, $\check{H}^p(U,F)=R^p\Gamma(F)$ directly and $\operatorname{Tot}$ collapse gives $R\Gamma(F^\bullet)$ as $\operatorname{Tot}$ with one filtration.
  For general $F^\bullet$ (e.g. de Rham $\Omega^\bullet_X$ on smooth $X$ with Hodge filtration) need $d_r$ for $r\geq 2$ and not declare $E_2=E_\infty$ by fiat.
  For concrete fibrations/pullbacks, Čech and Eilenberg-Moore interact: $\text{Čech}_{U}(R f_* F)$ via hypercohomology base-change $R\Gamma(X,F) \simeq R\Gamma(S,R f_*F)$.

* Requires: site effective covering families $\operatorname{CoveringFamily}(X,J)$ with $J$ via sieves already intaken plus $\operatorname{Nerve}: \operatorname{Covering}\to \mathsf{sSet}$ (Čech nerve); $\mathsf{Sh}(X)$ abelian with $\Gamma$ left exact and $R\Gamma$ via $D(\mathsf{Sh})$ injective already intaken in derived $R f_*$ lead; derived tensor $\otimes^L$ for Čech Alexander-Whitney / Eilenberg-Zilber on overlaps $U_{i_0\ldots}\times$; effective homology $\varepsilon: C\to \mathrm{EC}$ for each local $F(U_{i_0\ldots})$ via $\mathsf{EffHom}$ already intaken; model for hypercover $U_\bullet \to X$ Kan hypercover in $\mathsf{sSet}$/$\mathsf{Sites}$.
  Without $\mathsf{EffHom}$ and $D$, hyper/Čech remain table lookups.

Intended owners: `categories/sheaves/hypercohomology.py` (`Hypercohomology` with $R\Gamma: \mathsf{Ch}(\mathsf{Sh})\to D(\mathsf{Ab})$ $\to$ $H^i = H^i(R\Gamma)$, $R\Gamma(F^\bullet)$`.effective_complex()` via injective/$K$-injective + `BPL`), `categories/sheaves/cech.py` ($\operatorname{CechNerve}(U) : \mathsf{sSet}$, $\operatorname{CechComplex}(\check{C}^\bullet(U,F^\bullet)) : \mathsf{Ch}$, `CechToDerivedSpectralSequence` with $E_2^{p,q}=\check{H}^p(U, H^q(F^\bullet)) \Rightarrow H^{p+q}$), delegating to `categories/topology/effective_homology.py` (reductions for $\operatorname{Tot}$ perturbation), `categories/derived/derived_category.py` ($R\Gamma$ as $R\Gamma = R(p_*)$ from six-functor $R f_*$ lead), `categories/topology/sites.py` (`Site`, `CoveringFamily`, `Nerve`). Not free `hypercohomology(X,F)` or `cech_cohomology(cover,F)` lists — $F^\bullet$`.hypercohomology()` $\to R\Gamma(F^\bullet) : D$ with $F^\bullet$`.hypercohomology(i)` $\to H^i$, and `U.cech_complex(F)` $\to \check{C}^\bullet : \mathsf{Ch}^{\text{eff}}$ with $\check{H}^p$ and $\text{Čech}\to R\Gamma$ comparison as quasi-isomorphism when acyclic.

## Desired capability: filtered complexes, standard filtrations, Hodge-Frölicher and Grothendieck sseqs, with computable degeneration — note 2026-09-15

Plus filtered complexes, and the standard filtrations (e.g. the stupid filtration, Hodge filtrations, etc). One also needs the Hodge-Frölicher sseq and the sseqs from Grothendieck's seminal algebraic paper.
Ideally it should be possible to compute, sometimes, that a sseq degenerates on a specific page, purely from considerations regarding stabilization of differentials, zero or stabilized entries in certain gradings, etc.

* Filtered complex is not a bare $\mathsf{Ch}$ with a tag.
  Object $(K^\bullet, F) : \mathsf{FiltCh}(\mathsf{Ab})$ with $F^\bullet$ decreasing filtration $\cdots \supset F^p K^\bullet \supset F^{p+1} K^\bullet \supset \cdots$ by subcomplexes, exhaustive $\bigcup F^p=K$, separated $\bigcap F^p=0$ (or complete), finite or bounded variants; filtered maps $f: (K,F)\to(L,F)$ with $f(F^pK)\subset F^pL$.
  In non-abelian model stable setting, $\mathsf{Filt}(\mathsf{Sp})$ similarly.
  Interface `K.filtered()` and `K.filtration(p)` $\to F^pK : \mathsf{Ch}$ with $F^pK/F^{p+1}K = \operatorname{gr}_F^p K$ associated graded.

* Standard filtrations as functors, not ad-hoc integers:

  - Stupid/bête $\sigma_{\geq p}K$ / $\sigma_{\leq p}K$ (naive truncation): $(\sigma_{\geq p}K)^n = K^n$ if $n\geq p$ else $0$ (for decreasing) or $\sigma_{\leq p}$ dually; $\sigma$ is the filtration whose $\operatorname{gr}^p = K^p[-p]$ single degree.
    Provides `sseq` that collapses at $E_1 = K$.

  - Hodge filtration $F^p \Omega^{\bullet}_{X/k}$ on algebraic de Rham $\Omega^{\bullet}_{X/k}$ as $F^p = \Omega^{\geq p}$ (forms of degree $\geq p$), $\operatorname{gr}_F^p = \Omega^p[-p]$; conjugate filtration on same underlying `de Rham` with $F_{\text{conj}}$ via Cartier; weight filtration $W$ on mixed Hodge.
    Each is an object $\mathsf{FiltCh} \to \mathsf{FiltCh}$ with $\operatorname{gr}$ known.

* From a filtered complex, spectral sequence is object $E_r^{p,q}$ with $E_0^{p,q}=\operatorname{gr}_F^p K^{p+q}$, $E_1^{p,q}=H^{p+q}(\operatorname{gr}_F^p K)$, differentials $d_r^{p,q}: E_r^{p,q}\to E_r^{p+r,q-r+1}$ induced by $d_K$, pages $E_{r+1}=H(E_r,d_r)$, abutment $E_\infty^{p,q}=\operatorname{gr}_F^p H^{p+q}(K)$ as $\mathsf{GrMod}_{ZZ^2}$ with filtration quotients.
  Need `FilteredComplex.spectral_sequence() -> SpectralSequence` with `E_r(p,q)`, $d_r$ as morphisms $E_r^{p,q}\to E_r^{p+r,q-r+1}$ effective via $\mathrm{EC}$ when $\operatorname{gr}_F^p K$ and $K$ effective, and `E_r.next_page()`.

* Hodge-to-de Rham / Frölicher (Hodge–Frölicher) sseq is the $F$-sseq for $K= R\Gamma(X, \Omega^{\bullet}_{X/k})$ with Hodge filtration $F^pK = R\Gamma(X, F^p\Omega^{\bullet})$ ($F^p\Omega^{\bullet}=\Omega^{\geq p}$):

  $$
  E_1^{p,q}=H^q(X, \Omega^p_{X/k}) = H^{p+q}(\operatorname{gr}_F^p) \Rightarrow H^{p+q}_{\text{dR}}(X/k) = H^{p+q}(X, \Omega^{\bullet})
  $$

  $d_1 = d_{\text{dR}}$ on Hodge cohomology, higher $d_r$ are the Frölicher differentials.
  Operationalized and effective when $X : \mathsf{Sch}/k$ smooth proper with finite affine Čech $U$ and $\Omega^p(U_{i0\ldots})$ effective; then $\operatorname{gr}_F^p K = \operatorname{Tot}(\check{C}(U, \Omega^p[-p]))$ effective and $E_r$ via `FilteredComplex` above with $\mathrm{EC}$ for each $\operatorname{Tot}$.
  Degeneration at $E_1$ (`Hodge degeneration`) means $d_r=0$ for $r\geq 1$ as morphism, not as numeric coincidence; Frölicher inequality $\sum \dim E_1^{p,q} \geq \dim H_{\text{dR}}$ becomes testable via $\mathrm{EC}$ ranks.

* Grothendieck's sseqs from his Tohoku / Hartshorne Residues / Leray (his seminal algebraic paper) are composite-functor sseqs:

  - Grothendieck composite $\Gamma = G\circ F$ with $F: A\to B$ left exact sending injectives to $G$-acyclic $\to$ $E_2^{p,q}=R^pG(R^qF(A)) \Rightarrow R^{p+q}\Gamma(A)$ ($\mathsf{Filt}$ from Cartan-Eilenberg resolution $I^{\bullet,\bullet}$ of $F(I^\bullet)$).

  - Leray sseq for $f: X\to Y$ as instance $\Gamma = \Gamma(Y,-)\circ f_*$: $E_2^{p,q}=H^p(Y, R^qf_*F) \Rightarrow H^{p+q}(X,F)$ / $E_2^{p,q}=R^pf_*(R^qg_*F) \Rightarrow R^{p+q}(f\circ g)_*F$.

  - Local-to-global Ext $E_2^{p,q}=H^p(X, \operatorname{Ext}^q(F,G)) \Rightarrow \operatorname{Ext}^{p+q}(F,G)$ ($\Gamma = \operatorname{Hom}(F,-)\circ\Gamma$), and $\operatorname{Tor}$ analog $E^2_{p,q}=H_p(X, \operatorname{Tor}_q)$.
    Interface same `FilteredComplex` from double complex of injective resolutions $I^{p,q}$ / Čech double $\check{C}^{p}(U, I^q)$ — need $K$-injective $F^\bullet\to I^{\bullet,\bullet}$ effective when site finite.

* Computable degeneration on a specific page, purely from stabilization of differentials, zero or stabilized entries, etc., means the sseq object must expose decision procedures:

  ```text
  E_r.degenerates_at(r0) <=> forall r>=r0 forall p,q d_r^{p,q}=0 as morphism E_r^{p,q}->E_r^{p+r,q-r+1}
  E_r.collapses() <=> E_r = E_inf as GrMod
  periphery bounds: if E_r^{p,q}=0 for p<0 or q<0 or p>n or q>m (first-quadrant, etc.) then d_r^{p,q}=0 automatically for r large
  stabilization: if for fixed (p,q) the groups E_r^{p,q} stabilize (E_{r+1}^{p,q} ~= E_r^{p,q} via cycles = ker d_r and boundaries = im d_{r-?}) and all outgoing/incoming d_r into/out of that bidegree vanish because source/target is 0, then certify E_r^{p,q}=E_inf^{p,q}
  ```

  Implement as methods `E_r.is_zero(p,q)`, `E_r.differential_is_zero(p,q,r)` via $\mathrm{EC}$ matrix $d_r$ on $E_r^{p,q}$ computed from $\mathsf{Filt}$ cycles/boundaries (choose $\mathrm{EC}$ basis, compute $d_r$ as $\operatorname{Mat}_{\mathrm{rank}_{\text{target}} \times \mathrm{rank}_{\text{source}}}(R)$ and test $=0$ as morphism, not as $\operatorname{rank}$ equality).
  Then `degenerates_at(r0)` checks all $(p,q)$ in support box where $E_{r0}$ non-zero has $d_{\geq r0}=0$ because either matrix is zero or source/target zero — purely effective grading/zero-entry reasoning.
  Do not declare degeneration by a single coinciding dimension $\dim E_2 = \dim \text{abutment}$; need $d_r=0$ as maps.

* Effective weakens to exhaustive bounded filtrations (stupid, Hodge $0\leq p\leq \dim X$, weight finite) where $\mathsf{FiltCh}$ has finite length $\ell = \max p - \min p$ and $E_1$ has finite support box $0\leq p\leq \ell$, so degeneration test is finite over $\mathrm{EC}$ box; for unbounded/infinite-lengthfiltrations (complete, non-exhaustive) retain formal $E_r$ with known type but no finiteness claim.

* Requires: $\mathsf{FiltCh}$ with $F^p$ decreasing, $\operatorname{gr}_F^p$, $E_0$ already needed for hypercohomology/Čech/Serre/EM/Frölicher sharing one `SpectralSequence` type (no duplicate `FrolicherSS`); effective homology $\mathrm{EC}$ for $\operatorname{Tot}$ of $\check{C}(U, \operatorname{gr}_F^p)$ as above; model $R\Gamma$ via $D(\mathsf{Sh})$ for $X$ site; Hodge data $\Omega^p_{X/k} : \mathsf{QCoh}(X)$ with $F^p$ on $\Omega^{\bullet}$ as decreasing filtration by subcomplexes.

Intended owners: `categories/derived/filtered_complexes.py` (`FilteredComplex` with $F^p$, $\operatorname{gr}_F^p$, $E_0/E_1$, `spectral_sequence() -> SpectralSequence`), `categories/hodge/hodge_filtration.py` (`HodgeFiltration` as $F^p\Omega^{\bullet}=\Omega^{\geq p}$ with $\operatorname{gr}$, $F^pR\Gamma$ as `Filtered`), `categories/schemes/hodge_frolicher.py` (`FrolicherSS` as $E_1^{p,q}=H^q(X,\Omega^p) \Rightarrow H^{p+q}_{\text{dR}}$ via `FilteredComplex`, delegating to filtered $\mathrm{EC}$), `categories/homotopy/spectral_sequences.py` (`SpectralSequence` with `E_r(p,q)`, $d_r$, `is_zero`, `degenerates_at(r0)`, `stabilizes_at(p,q,r0)`), `categories/sheaves/grothendieck_sseq.py` (`GrothendieckSpectralSequence` / `LeraySS` as $E_2^{p,q}=R^pG(R^qF) \Rightarrow R^{p+q}\Gamma$ from Cartan-Eilenberg/Čech double).
Not a free `hodge_sseq(X)` list — `X.filtered_de_rham().spectral_sequence()` and `U.cech_double(F_bullet).filtered()` on filtered objects, with degeneration tested as morphism-zero via $\mathrm{EC}$ matrices, not dimension coincidence.

## Intake: https://www-fourier.univ-grenoble-alpes.fr/~sergerar/Papers/Ana-JSC.pdf — fixtures + algorithms — 2026-09-15 — verbatim

Paper is Ana Romero–Julio Rubio–Francis Sergeraert–Alberto Alzola *Effective homology of filtered complexes*, JSC (Kenzo team).
Provides explicit test fixtures and algorithms for the filtered effective-homology sseq machinery already intaken via Kenzo; does not introduce a new mathematical theory beyond §3 definitions but supplies oracles a preamble must reproduce.

**What it contains (verbatim extraction for fixtures):**

*Theorem 15 (p.7):* Let $C$ filtered with effective homology $(\mathrm{HC}, \varepsilon)$, $\varepsilon=(D,\rho,\rho')$, $\rho=(f,g,h)$, $\rho'=(f',g',h')$.
If filtrations also on $\mathrm{HC},D$ and $f,f',g,g'$ filtered and $h,h'$ have order $\leq t$ ($h(F_pD)\subset F_{p+t}D$ $\forall p$), then spectral sequences $E_r(C) \cong E_r(\mathrm{HC})$ for $r>t$.
Proof uses $f_r g_r=\mathrm{id}$ and $h: gf\simeq \mathrm{id} \Rightarrow (gf)_r=(\mathrm{id})_r$ for $r>t$ via [MacLane Homology Prop.3.5 p.331].

*Definitions:* filtration $F_pC\subset C$ with $d(F_p)\subset F_p$, bounded $F_sC_n=0, F_tC_n=C_n$ for $s<t$ per $n$; Z-bigraded $E=\{E_{p,q}\}$, differential $d:E\to E$ bidegree $(-r,r-1)$, spectral sequence $E=\{E_r,d_r\}$ with $H(E_r,d_r)\cong E_{r+1}$; $Z^r_{p,q}=\{a\in F_pC_{p+q} \mid d(a)\in F_{p-r}C_{p+q-1}\}$, $E^r_{p,q}=Z^r_{p,q}/(dZ^{r-1}_{p+r,q-r+1}\cup F_{p-1}C_{p+q})$, $d_r$ induced by $d$ (Thm.8). Note: formal expression not sufficient when $Z^r_{p,q}$ not finite type — needs effective homology (p.6).

*Effective homology:* reduction $\rho:D\Rightarrow C$ is $(f:D\to C, g:C\to D, h:D\to D[1])$ with $fg=\mathrm{id}_C, gf+d h+ h d=\mathrm{id}_D, f h=0, h g=0, h h=0$ (Def.9, Rem.10 $D=\operatorname{Ker} f\oplus\operatorname{Im} g$, $H(D)\cong H(C)$). Strong equivalence $C\Leftrightarrow E$ via $C\Leftarrow D\Rightarrow E$.
Object with effective homology $(X,\mathrm{HC},\varepsilon)$ with $\mathrm{HC}$ effective (each $\mathrm{HC}_n$ finitely generated free with algorithm for Z-basis, [9]) and $\varepsilon: C_*(X)\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{HC}$ (Def.12). Example 13 (Serre): $G\hookrightarrow E\to B$ with $E=B\times_\tau G$, $C(B\times G) \Rightarrow C(B)\otimes C(G)$ EZ reduction + BPL with twisting $\tau$ gives $C(B\times_\tau G)\Rightarrow C(B)\otimes_t C(G)$; with $C(B)\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{HB}$, $C(G)\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{HG}$ gives $C(B)\otimes_t C(G)\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{HB}\otimes_t \mathrm{HG}$ effective via second BPL; composite is $\varepsilon_E$.
Example 14 (EM): $\Omega X$ via cobar on coalgebra.

*Kenzo objects:* `kz1` $= K(Z,1)$ as $K(Z,1)_n=Z^n(\Delta^n,Z)=Z^n$ (minimal model, locally effective, `K1Abelian-Simplicial-Group`), `basiskz1 3` error locally-effective, `efhm(kz1)=[K22 Homotopy-Equivalence K1<=K?=>K16]` with `orgn(K16)=(CIRCLE)`, `basis(K16) 0=(*) 1=(S1)`, `?(K16) 1 S1 =0` $\Rightarrow H_0=H_1=Z$.

*Class `Filtered-Complex : Chain-Complex` with slot `flin: (degr, gen)` $\mapsto p=\min\{t \mid \text{gen}\in F_tC\}$.
Functions: `build-FltrChcm :cmpr :basis :bsgn :intr-dffr :dffr-strt :flin :orgn`, `change-chcm-to-FltrChcm(chcm, flin, orgn)`, `fltrd-basis(fltrcm,degr,fltr-index)` (=basis of $F_pC_n$), `fltr-chcm-dffr-mtrx(fltrcm,degr,fltr-index)` (=matrix of $d: F_pC_n\to F_pC_{n-1}$). Sseq core: `print-spct-sqn-cmpns(fltrcm,r,p,q)` (components $Z$/$Z_m$), `spct-sqn-basis-dvs(fltrcm,r,p,q)` (numerator generators vs denominator $dZ^{r-1}\cup F_{p-1}$), `spct-sqn-dffr(fltrcm,r,p,q,int-list)` ($d_r$ on coordinate list, e.g. `(1)` for generator $(s2,\eta_1\eta_0[])$ maps to `(1)` = $(\eta_0 *,[1])$), `spct-sqn-cnvg-level(fltrcm,degr)` (smallest $r$ with $E_{p,q}^\infty=E_{p,q}^r$ for $p+q=\text{degr}$).

*Section 6 didactic fixtures (hand-computable, for verbatim oracles):*

- *6.1 $S^2\times_\tau K(Z,1)$* $\tau:S^2\to K(Z,1)$ $\tau(s2)=[1]$ (if $[2]$ then $P^3R$). `s2=sphere2` `[K23]`, `kz1=K(Z,1)` `[K1]`, `tau=build-smmr :src s2 :trg kz1 :degr -1 :sintr lambda(_,_)->absm 0 '(1)`, total `s2-tw1-kz1=fibration-total(tau)` `[K34]`. Effective `rbcc(efhm(s2-tw1-kz1))=[K95]` with `orgn=(ADD[K74][K93(degree -1)])`, `K74 = TNSR-PRDC[K23][K16]` = $S^2\otimes S^1$.
  So effective is $S^2\otimes_t S^1$ perturbed differential.
  Filtrations: `twpr-flin(degr,crpr)= -degr + length(dgop-int-ext(dgop))` i.e. degeneracy degree w.r.t. base (count $s_i$ in second factor), implemented as `#'(lambda(degr,crpr) (- degr (length (dgop-int-ext (Car crpr))))...)`; `tnpr-flin(degr,tnpr)=degr1(tnpr)` = base dimension on tensor product $F_p(C(B)\otimes C(G))=\bigoplus_{m\leq p}C(B)_m\otimes C(G)$.
  Then `change-chcm-to-FltrChcm(s2-tw1-kz1, twpr-flin)` `[K34Filtered]` and `change-chcm-to-FltrChcm(s2xts1, tnpr-flin)` `[K95Filtered]`. Homotopies order 0 $\Rightarrow E_r(\text{s2-tw1-kz1})\cong E_r(s2\otimes_t S^1)$ for all $r$.
  Oracles: $E^2_{2,0}=Z$ generator $-1\cdot(s2, \eta_1\eta_0[])$ (0 denominator), $E^2_{0,1}=Z$ generator $-1\cdot(\eta_0 *,[1])$, $d^2_{2,0}(1)=(1)$ i.e. $(s2,\eta_1\eta_0[])\mapsto(\eta_0 *,[1])$, hence $E^3_{0,1}=0, E^3_{2,0}=0$, convergence $r=1$ for total degree 0 and $r=3$ for degree 1 (`spct-sqn-cnvg-level` $0\to 1$, $1\to 3$), abutment $E^1_{0,0}=Z$ only.

- *6.2 $S^2\times_\tau K(Z/2,1)$* same base with `kz21=K(Z/2,1)` `[K110]` where non-degenerate $n$-simplex is integer $n$ representing sequence $1^n$, void `[]` =0; `tau2` same with `absm 0 1` and total `s2-tw2-kz21` `[K128Filtered]`. Now finite type (no effective bypass needed) but same filtration.
  Oracles: $E^2_{0,1}=Z/2$ ($(\eta_0 *,1)$ with divisor $2$), $E^2_{2,0}=Z$ (numerator 5 gens, denominator 4 gens, surviving $(s2,\eta_1\eta_00)$), $E^2_{0,3}=Z/2$, $d^2_{2,0}(1)=(1)$ i.e. $(s2,\eta_1\eta_00)\mapsto(\eta_0 *,1)$ again, convergence levels $\text{deg}1\to 3, \text{deg}2\to 1, \text{deg}3\to 1$.

*Section 7 advanced fixtures (beyond literature, for stress tests):*

- *7.1 Postnikov tower `X4`* with $\pi_i=Z/2$ at each stage and simplest nontrivial invariants (`[10 pp.142-145]`): `X2=K(Z/2,2)` `[K133]`, `k3=chml-clss(X2,4)` `[K245]` on `K150`, `F3=z2-whitehead(X2,k3)` `[K260]`, `X3=fibration-total(F3)` `[K266]`, `k4=chml-clss(X3,5)` `[K479]`, `F4=z2-whitehead(X3,k4)` `[K494]`, `X4=fibration-total(F4)` `[K500]`, `effX4=rbcc(efhm(X4))` `[K696]`. As twisted products $K(Z/2,4)\times_{k4}X3$ where $X3=K(Z,3)\times_{k3}K(Z/2,2)$.
  Filtrations as before (`fbrt-flin` on total, `tnpr-flin` on effective).
  Oracles for $r=2$: $E^2_{0,4}=Z/2, E^2_{5,0}=Z/4, E^2_{6,0}=Z/2\oplus Z/2$ and for $p+q=4..7$ convergence at $r=6$; differentials $d^5_{5,0}(1)=(1): Z/4\to Z/2$ ($E^5_{5,0}\to E^5_{0,4}$) and $d^5_{7,0}(1)=(1): Z/2\to Z/2$ ($E^5_{7,0}\to E^5_{2,4}$); $E^6_{0,7}=Z/2, E^6_{3,4}=Z/2$ else 0 in total degree 7.

- *7.2 Eilenberg-Moore $X$ vs $\Omega X$* for $m$-reduced $X$ with effective homology, $\Omega X$ via cobar, iterated $\Omega^k$ for $k\leq m$; filtration is cobar filtration on $B(C_*(X))$.
  Figures: Fig.1 $E_\infty^{p,q}$ $q-p\leq 8$ for $\Omega S^3$ vs $\Omega\Omega S^3$ and Fig.2 for $\Omega S^3\cup_2 D^3$ vs $\Omega(\Omega S^3\cup_2 D^3)$ with tables of $Z$, $Z/2, Z/3, Z/5, Z/6, Z/10$ components as listed pp.14-15 — exact effective homology oracles for $\Omega$ attachment.

**Additional algorithms beyond Kenzo core (p.15):** exact couples `G.W.Whitehead [12]` as more general than filtered complexes — a filtered complex determines an exact couple whose sseq is that of the filtered complex, but not conversely; Bousfield-Kan sseq does not arise from a filtration.
Paper proposes future programs for exact couples analogously to `Filtered-Complex` $\Rightarrow E_r$ via exact couple $D\leftrightarrow E$ with $d_r$.

**Use:** intake already owns `FilteredComplex(flin)`, `SpectralSequence(E_r,d_r)` with `degenerates_at` etc.; this paper supplies the executable `flin` definitions (`twpr-flin`, `tnpr-flin`), the `change-chcm-to-FltrChcm` adapter contract, the perturbation-order theorem that justifies computing $E_{>t}$ on $\mathrm{HC}$, and the explicit $E_r^{p,q}$ and $d_r$ fixture values above for $S^2\times_\tau K(Z,1)$ etc. that any preamble adapter must reproduce as $\mathsf{GrMod}$ specimens.

## Desired capability: interactive spectral sequence visualizer attached to sseq objects — note 2026-09-15

Note the need for an interactive spectral sequence visualizer attached to sseq objects.
Should be able to show each page, differentials toggleable, entries in each bigraded slot, allow flipping pages, have tooltips with additional mathematical information.

* Visualizer is not a detached notebook helper.
  It is a method/view attached to the sseq object `E = SpectralSequence(E_r,d_r)` from `FilteredComplex`, `EilenbergMoore`, `Serre`, `Hodge-Frolicher`, `Grothendieck` etc.: `E.visualize()` / `E.show()` / `E._repr_html_()` returns an interactive widget bound to that $E$, not a free `plot_sseq(E)` with duplicated state.
  State lives on $E$; the widget reads $E_r^{p,q}$, $d_r$, `E_r.next_page()`, `converges`, `degenerates_at`, and $\mathrm{EC}$ evidence.

* Per-page grid: bigraded $(p,q)$ plane as interactive grid/table where each cell $E_r^{p,q}$ displays its $\mathsf{GrMod}$ value (e.g. $Z$, $Z/2$, $Z/4$, $Z/2\oplus Z/2$, $0$, free rank + torsion invariants from `Smith` as effective $\mathrm{EC}$) with compact label and color for zero vs non-zero vs stabilized.
  Axes $p$ (filtration) horizontal, $q$ complementary vertical, with bounds derived from $\mathsf{FiltCh}$ support box (e.g. $0\leq p\leq \dim B$ for Serre fibre of dim B).

* Bigraded entries: each slot $E_r^{p,q}$ is a $\mathsf{GrMod}$ object (often $\mathsf{Ab} = Z\text{-mod}$) with presentation $Z^{r}\oplus\bigoplus Z/d_i$ from `Smith` on $\mathrm{EC}$ differential matrices; cell shows summary $Z^a\oplus Z/2^b\oplus...$ and expands to generators/relations on demand.
  For effective $\mathrm{EC}$, entries are computable matrices, not formal symbols.

* Differentials toggleable: $d_r^{p,q}: E_r^{p,q}\to E_r^{p+r,q-r+1}$ as arrows on grid; toggle per $r$ ($d_1$, $d_2$, $\ldots$) on/off, with arrow opacity/thickness reflecting rank, and click to highlight kernel $Z_r^{p,q}$ vs image $B_r^{p,q}$.
  $d_r$ matrices come from `E.filtered_complex().differential(r,p,q)` via $\mathrm{EC}$ (via `spct-sqn-dffr` analog); zero $d_r$ shown dashed.

* Page flipping: controls to advance $E_r \to E_{r+1}=H(E_r,d_r)$ with animation/morph of cells (surviving $Z_r/B_r$), back/forward, jump to $E_2$, $E_\infty$, and $r$-slider with `convergence level` $r_\infty(p+q)$ per total degree from `spct-sqn-cnvg-level` analog (`E.degenerates_at(r0)`, `E.stabilizes_at(p,q,r0)`). $E_\infty^{p,q}=F^pH^{p+q}/F^{p+1}$ abutment shown as final page with filtration quotients linking to $H^{p+q}(X)$ via $\mathrm{EC}(X)$.

* Tooltips with additional mathematical information: hover/click on $E_r^{p,q}$ shows $Z^r_{p,q}$ numerator vs $dZ^{r-1}_{p+r,q-r+1}\cup F_{p-1}C_{p+q}$ denominator (exactly the `spct-sqn-basis-dvs` data: numerator generator combinations as `CrPr`/`CmBn` symbols from $K(Z,n)$ etc. and denominator divisors), filtration index $p=\min\{t \mid \text{gen}\in F_t\}$, degree $p+q$, $d_r$ matrix on basis, cycles $\ker d_r$ vs boundaries $\operatorname{im} d_r$, and convergence provenance (e.g. $S^2\times_\tau K(Z,1)\ d^2_{2,0}: (s2,\eta_1\eta_0[])\mapsto(\eta_0 *,[1])$ with coordinate $(1)\mapsto(1)$). For `k-invariant` pages, tooltip links $\kappa_n\in H^{n+2}(X^{(n)};\pi_{n+1})$ classifying $K(\pi,n+1)\hookrightarrow X^{(n+1)}\to X^{(n)}$.

* Interaction contracts: widget subscribes to $E$ (no duplicate state), $E$ remains effective via $\mathrm{EC}$ reductions; widget works in Jupyter via `_repr_html_`/`ipywidgets`/Bokeh/Plotly or Quarto HTML export without server, and degrades to ASCII table when headless.
  Reuses `SpectralSequence` owners already intaken (`categories/homotopy/spectral_sequences.py` with $E_r$, $d_r$, `E_r.next_page()`), `categories/derived/filtered_complexes.py` (`flin` degeneracy degree `twpr-flin` etc.), and `categories/topology/effective_homology.py` ($\mathrm{EC}$ Smith); does not reimplement $E_r$ logic in JS.

Intended owners: `categories/homotopy/spectral_sequences.py` (`SpectralSequence.visualize() -> InteractiveSseq` widget bound to $E$, with `show_page(r)`, `toggle_differential(r)`, `cell_tooltip(p,q)` reading $E_r^{p,q}$ as $\mathsf{GrMod}$ and $d_r$ as $\mathsf{Mat}$), delegating to `src/dzack_research/preamble/visualization/sseq.py` or `notebooks/visualizer` adapter for rendering (grid, arrows, tooltips), and to `categories/derived/filtered_complexes.py` for `flin` metadata.
Not a detached `plot_sseq(E)` function returning static PNG — `E.visualize()` on the sseq object with page state, bigraded slots, and tooltips driven by `EC` differentials.

## Desired capability: concrete geometric models for classifying spaces $BG$, $EG\to BG$, simplicial objects in $C$, configuration spaces — note 2026-09-15

Note the need for concrete geometric representations of many classifying spaces $BG$.
E.g. $RP^\infty$, $CP^\infty$, lens spaces $L_p^\infty$, $F_n$ the free group on $n$ gens, $\operatorname{Gr}_n(C^\infty)$, $\operatorname{Gr}_n(R^\infty)$, $B\mathrm{SO}_n$, $B\mathrm{Sp}_n$, $B\mathrm{GL}_n$, $X = B\pi_1(X)$ in good cases, $BG$ for the (pure) braid groups $B_n$ and $P_n$, $B\mathrm{Spin}_n$, $B\mathrm{String}_n$, $HP^\infty$, needs $HH$ and $HH^\infty$ to exist, needs to understand $B(G\times H)$, the associated bundle construction, and the fibrations $EG\to BG$ for all of these.
Should also be able to construct $EG$ and $BG$ explicitly categorically and simplicially.
Needs an honest way to construct simplicial objects in $C$, and particularly simplicial sets.
Needs various configuration spaces: ordered, unordered, allowing points to coincide vs not, etc.

* $BG$ is not a formal symbol $B(G)$.
  For $G : \mathsf{Groups}$ (discrete), topological group, Lie group, simplicial group, $BG : \mathsf{HoTop}$ is the classifying space with $\Omega BG \simeq G$ as $H$-group and $\pi_{i+1}(BG)\cong\pi_i(G)$, $\pi_0(BG)=\ast$ (connected), universal principal $G$-bundle $EG\to BG$ with $EG$ contractible and $G$ free action.
  For discrete $G$, $BG = K(G,1)$.
  Interface must be `G.classifying_space() -> BG : HoTop` with $EG : \mathsf{Top}$ and $p: EG\to BG$ Kan/Serre fibration with fiber $G$, not a bare list of cells.

* Concrete models (each is an object $BG : \mathsf{HoTop}$ with effective homology when $G$ finite type, plus $EG\to BG$ fibration with $EG$ contractible):

  - $RP^\infty = B(Z/2) = K(Z/2,1)$ as $\operatorname{colim}_n RP^n$ with $RP^n = S^n/(x\sim -x)$ and cellular $e_0\cup e_1\cup\ldots$ one cell each dimension, $H^*(RP^\infty;F_2)=F_2[w_1]$ $|w_1|=1$ with $w_1$ universal Stiefel-Whitney; $EG=S^\infty$.

  - $CP^\infty = B U(1)=B S^1 = K(Z,2)$ as $\operatorname{colim}_n CP^n$, $H^*(CP^\infty;Z)=Z[c_1]$ $|c_1|=2$, $EG=S^\infty$ with $S^1$ Hopf $S^\infty\to CP^\infty$; also $CP^\infty=\operatorname{Gr}_1(C^\infty)$.

  - Lens $L_p^\infty = B(Z/p)$ as $S^\infty/(z\sim\zeta_p z)$ with $\zeta_p=e^{2\pi i/p}$, $H^*(L_p^\infty;F_p)=F_p[x]\otimes\Lambda(y)$ $|y|=1,|x|=2, \beta y=x$, universal $Z/p$ bundle $S^\infty\to L_p^\infty$; needs $S^\infty$ as simplicial $EG$ already.

  - $F_n$ free group on $n$ gens: $BF_n = \vee^n S^1$ wedge of $n$ circles (graph with one vertex, $n$ loops), $\pi_1(BF_n)=F_n$, higher $\pi=0$; $EF_n$ is $n$-regular tree (Cayley graph of $F_n$) contractible.

  - $\operatorname{Gr}_n(C^\infty)=B U(n)$, $\operatorname{Gr}_n(R^\infty)=B O(n)$ Grassmannians as $\operatorname{colim}_k \operatorname{Gr}_n(C^k)$ / $\operatorname{colim}_k \operatorname{Gr}_n(R^k)$ with Schubert cells, $H^*(BO(n);F_2)=F_2[w_1,\ldots,w_n]$, $H^*(BU(n);Z)=Z[c_1,\ldots,c_n]$; $EG=V_n(C^\infty)=\text{Stiefel manifold}$ $V_n(C^\infty)=U(\infty)/U(\infty-n)$ contractible with $O(n)$/$U(n)$ free.

  - $B\mathrm{SO}_n$, $B\mathrm{Sp}_n = B\mathrm{Sp}(n)$, $B\mathrm{GL}_n = B\mathrm{GL}_n(C)$ / $B\mathrm{GL}_n(R)$ variants: $B\mathrm{SO}_n = \operatorname{Gr}_n^+(R^\infty)$ oriented Grassmannian (double cover of $\operatorname{Gr}_n(R^\infty)$), $B\mathrm{Sp}_n$ quaternionic $\operatorname{Gr}_n(H^\infty)$, $B\mathrm{GL}_n$ algebraic vs topological (need $\mathsf{Top}$ vs $\mathsf{Alg}$ model).
    All via Stiefel $EG\to BG$ with $EG$ Stiefel $V_n$.

  - $X = B\pi_1(X)$ good cases: when $X$ aspherical ($\pi_{>1}=0$), e.g. $X$ closed surface of genus $g\geq 1$, $X$ $K(\pi,1)$ already, so $X \simeq B\pi_1(X)$ as $\mathsf{HoTop}$ equivalence (not formal).

  - $BG$ for braid $B_n$ and pure $P_n$: $B_n = \pi_1(\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{unord}}(C))$ with $\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{unord}}(C)=\operatorname{Conf}_n(C)/\Sigma_n$, $P_n=\pi_1(\operatorname{Conf}_n(C))$; models $\operatorname{Conf}_n(C)=C^n\setminus\Delta$ complements of arrangement $\Delta=\bigcup_{i<j}\{z_i=z_j\}$; $BB_n = \operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{unord}}(C) \simeq K(B_n,1)$ aspherical (Fadell-Neuwirth fibrations).
    Needs $\operatorname{Conf}$ intaken below.

  - $B\mathrm{Spin}_n$, $B\mathrm{String}_n$ higher connected covers of $BO_n$: $B\mathrm{Spin}_n = B\mathrm{SO}_n$ with $w_2$ killed ($\pi_1$ cover), $B\mathrm{String}_n$ with $(1/2)p_1$ killed ($\pi_3$); as homotopy fibers of $w_2: B\mathrm{SO}_n\to K(Z/2,2)$ and $(1/2)p_1: B\mathrm{Spin}_n\to K(Z,4)$.
    Needs $K(G,n)$ effective already.

  - $HP^\infty = B\mathrm{Sp}(1)=BS^3 = K?$ $HP^\infty = \operatorname{Gr}_1(H^\infty) = S^\infty/S^3$ with $H^*(HP^\infty;Z)=Z[u]$ $|u|=4$, $EG=S^\infty$; $HH$ $=H$ and $HH^\infty$ $=B?$ (quaternionic).
    Need $H = \{\text{quaternions}\}$ as $\mathsf{NormedDivAlg}$.

* $HH$ and $HH^\infty$ to exist: $H = \text{Hamilton quaternions}$ as $R$-algebra $H = R\langle i,j \mid i^2=j^2=-1, ij=-ji=k\rangle$ object of $\mathsf{Alg}_R$ with $|H:R|=4$, norm $N: H\to R$, $\mathrm{Sp}(1)=S^3 = \{q\in H \mid N(q)=1\}$ as Lie group $G : \mathsf{Groups}$ with $\operatorname{Lie}(G)=\operatorname{im} H$.
  Then $HP^n = \operatorname{Gr}_1(H^{n+1})$ and $HP^\infty = \operatorname{colim} HP^n = B\mathrm{Sp}(1)$.
  $HH^\infty$ is infinite quaternionic projective limit / $B\mathrm{Sp}(\infty)=B\mathrm{Sp}$.

* $B(G\times H)$ understanding: for $G,H : \mathsf{Groups}$, $B(G\times H) \simeq BG \times BH$ as $\mathsf{HoTop}$ with $EG\times H = EG\times EH$, projections give $B(G\times H)=E(G\times H)/(G\times H)$.
  More generally $B$ preserves products $B: \mathsf{Grp}(\mathsf{Top})\to\mathsf{HoTop}$ product-preserving.
  Associated bundle construction: given principal $G$-bundle $P\to X$ ($P = f^*EG$ for $f:X\to BG$) and left $G$-space $F$, associated $P\times_G F = (P\times F)/G \to X$ with fiber $F$, transition $\tau: U_{ij}\to G$ acting on $F$.
  Interface `P.associated_bundle(F) -> E : Top` with $E = P\times_G F$.

* Fibrations $EG\to BG$ for all: principal bundle with fiber $G$, structure map $EG = \bar{W}?$?
  Models: $EG = \bar{W}?$ Choose: $EG = |W G|$ geometric realization of simplicial $WG$ (Kan's $WG_n = G_n\times\ldots\times G_0$) contractible with free $G$-action $WG\times G\to WG$, $BG = \bar{W}G = WG/G$.
  Or $EG = \operatorname{colim} \operatorname{Gr}$ Stiefel model for matrix groups.
  Need both explicit categorical and simplicial constructions of $EG$ and $BG$ as objects $EG : \mathsf{sSet}$ / $\mathsf{Top}$ with $G$-action and $BG = EG/G$ quotient.

* Honest simplicial objects in $C$: for any category $C$ (here $C=\mathsf{Top}, \mathsf{Sets}, \mathsf{Groups}, \mathsf{Mod}_R, \mathsf{Alg}, \text{etc.}$), $sC = \operatorname{Fun}(\Delta^{op}, C)$ simplicial objects $X_\bullet$ with $X_n : C$, face $d_i: X_n\to X_{n-1}$, degeneracy $s_i: X_n\to X_{n+1}$ satisfying simplicial identities $d_i d_j = d_{j-1}d_i \ (i<j)$ etc. Interface `SimplicialObject(C)` with $X : sC$ and $C(X)_*: \mathsf{Ch}$ via Dold-Kan when $C$ abelian.
  Specialization to $C=\mathsf{Sets}$ gives $\mathsf{sSet}$ as already intaken $\mathsf{sSet} = \mathsf{sSets}$, but now as instance of $sC$.
  Needs $\Delta$ as simplex category $\Delta(n,m)=\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathsf{Ord}}([n],[m])$ and $\operatorname{Nerve}: \mathsf{Cat}\to\mathsf{sSet}$ as $N(C)_n = \operatorname{Fun}([n],C)$.

* Configuration spaces: for $X : \mathsf{Top}$ (e.g. $X=R^2=C, R^n, \text{manifold } M$), ordered $\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{ord}}(X)=X^n\setminus\Delta$ where $\Delta = \{(x_i) \mid \exists i\neq j\ x_i=x_j\}$ fat diagonal, $\Sigma_n$-action permuting coordinates; unordered $\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{unord}}(X)=\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{ord}}(X)/\Sigma_n$ quotient by free $\Sigma_n$ when $X$ Hausdorff and points distinct, and more generally $\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\leq}(X)=X^n$ allowing collisions (fat diagonal kept) vs $\operatorname{Conf}_n^{<}(X)=\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{ord}}$ not allowing.
  Generalizations: $\operatorname{Conf}_{n,k}(X)$ allowing at most $k$ coincidences, and $\operatorname{Conf}_n(X; \leq m)$ bounded multiplicity.
  For $X$ manifold, Fadell-Neuwirth fibration $\operatorname{Conf}_{n+1}(X)\to\operatorname{Conf}_n(X)$ with fiber $X \setminus \{n \text{ points}\}$ when $X$ connected manifold $\dim\geq 2$.
  Ordered vs unordered $B_n, P_n$ already above via $\operatorname{Conf}_n(C)$.
  Need models: complements $R^n\setminus\Delta$ as semialgebraic $\mathsf{Top}$, simplicial $\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\Delta}$ via $\mathsf{sSet}$ triangulation of $X^n$ minus $\Delta$, and effective homology for $\operatorname{Conf}_n$ (when $X\ \mathsf{sSet}$ effective, $\operatorname{Conf}_n$ inherits `EffHom` via product $X^n$ and `EffHom` of complement as subspace, not formal).

Intended owners: `categories/topology/classifying_spaces.py` (`ClassifyingSpace(G) -> BG : HoTop` with $EG\to BG$ $G$-bundle, $B: \mathsf{Grp}\to\mathsf{HoTop}$ product-preserving, plus concrete models `RP_infty=K(Z/2,1)`, `CP_infty=K(Z,2)`, `Lens_p`, $\operatorname{Gr}_n(C^\infty)=B U(n)$/$\operatorname{Gr}_n(R^\infty)=B O(n)$/$\operatorname{Gr}_n^+(R^\infty)=B \mathrm{SO}_n$, $B\mathrm{Sp}_n$, $B\mathrm{GL}_n$, $B\mathrm{Spin}_n$, $B\mathrm{String}_n$, `HP_infty=BSp(1)` and $HH$, $BF_n=\vee^n S^1$, $BB_n=\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{unord}}(C)$, $BP_n=\operatorname{Conf}_n(C)$ as $K(B_n,1)/K(P_n,1)$, $B(G\times H)\cong BG\times BH$, associated $P\times_G F$), delegating to `categories/algebras/division_algebras.py` ($H$, $\mathrm{Sp}(1)$), `categories/topology/simplicial_sets.py` ($\mathsf{sSet}$, $\Delta$), `categories/topology/simplicial_objects.py` ($sC = \operatorname{Fun}(\Delta^{op},C)$ with $\Delta$ simplex category, `Nerve`, `Realization`), `categories/topology/configuration_spaces.py` ($\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{ord}}(X)$, $\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{unord}}(X)=\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\text{ord}}/\Sigma_n$, $\operatorname{Conf}_n^{\leq}=X^n$, Fadell-Neuwirth fibrations), `categories/topology/effective_homology.py` (`EC` for $\operatorname{Conf}_n$ via `EC(X)^n` and complement).
Not a free `classifying_space(G)` returning bare list — `G.classifying_space() -> BG` with `G.universal_bundle() -> EG->BG` as $G$-space fibration object.

## Desired capability: character varieties, Betti moduli space, general GIT quotients and Hilbert schemes, honest holonomy groups — note 2026-09-15

Note the need for character varieties, the Betti moduli space.
Need general GIT quotients and Hilbert schemes.
Need honest holonomy groups.

* Character variety is not a bare $\operatorname{Hom}(\pi,G)/G$ set.
  For $\pi : \mathsf{Groups}$ (e.g. $\pi=\pi_1(X,x) : \mathsf{Groups}$ finitely presented $\pi=\langle S\mid R\rangle$, or $\pi=\pi_1^{\text{ét}}(X)$, $\operatorname{Gal}_K$) and $G : \mathsf{GrpSch}/k$ (reductive, e.g. $\mathrm{GL}_n, \mathrm{SL}_n, \mathrm{PGL}_n, \mathrm{Sp}_{2n}, O_n, G_2$, or algebraic torus), $\operatorname{Rep}(\pi,G)=\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathsf{Groups}}(\pi,G) : \mathsf{Sch}/k$ is the representation scheme with $\operatorname{Rep}(\pi,G)(A)=\operatorname{Hom}(\pi,G(A))$ as $A$-points; for $\pi=\langle S\mid R\rangle$, $\operatorname{Rep} = \{(g_s)\in G^S \mid r((g_s))=1\ \forall r\in R\} \subset G^S$ closed subscheme via equations $r=1$ in $G$.
  Character variety $X(\pi,G)=\operatorname{Rep}(\pi,G)/\!/G : \mathsf{Sch}/k$ is the GIT quotient by conjugation $G$ acting by $g\cdot\rho = g\rho g^{-1}$ (conjugation on each generator).
  Betti moduli $M_B(X,G)=X(\pi_1(X),G)$ for $X : \mathsf{Sch}/C$ smooth with $U=X^{an}$ is the Betti component of nonabelian Hodge: $M_B = \operatorname{Hom}(\pi_1(U),G)/\!/G$ as categorical quotient $\operatorname{Spec}(k[\operatorname{Rep}]^G)$ (affine).
  Distinguish $\operatorname{Rep}^s$ stable, $\operatorname{Rep}^{irr}$ irreducible, $X^{irr}=\operatorname{Rep}^{irr}/G$ geometric quotient; $X(\pi,G)(k)$ points are $G(k)$-conjugacy classes of semisimple representations.
  Interface `pi.character_variety(G) -> X(pi,G) : Sch` and `X.betti_moduli(G) -> M_B` on the space, not free `character_variety(pi,G)` with matrices.

* Needs GIT quotients generally: for $X : \mathsf{Sch}/k$ with linearized $G$-action ($G : \mathsf{GrpSch}$ reductive acting via $\sigma: G\times X\to X$ and $L : \operatorname{Pic}^G(X)$ ample linearization), $X/\!/_L G = \operatorname{Proj}(\bigoplus_{n\geq 0} \Gamma(X,L^{\otimes n})^G) : \mathsf{Sch}/k$ categorical quotient with $\pi: X^{ss}\to X/\!/G$ ($X^{ss}$ semistable as $\exists s\in\Gamma(X,L^{\otimes n})^G, s(x)\neq 0$ and $X_s$ affine), and $X^s\subset X^{ss}$ stable with $\pi|_{X^s}: X^s\to X^s/G$ geometric quotient (free action, orbits closed).
  For affine $X=\operatorname{Spec} A$ with $G$ reductive, $X/\!/G = \operatorname{Spec}(A^G)$ via invariants $A^G = \{a\in A \mid g\cdot a=a\}$.
  Quotient is object `GITQuotient(X,G,L) : Sch` with $\pi: X^{ss}\to X/\!/G$ morphism, not a set quotient $X/G$.

* Needs Hilbert schemes generally: for $X : \mathsf{Sch}/k$ projective with $O(1)$ ample, $\operatorname{Hilb}^P(X) : \mathsf{Sch}/k$ is the Hilbert scheme parametrizing flat families $Z\subset X\times S$ with Hilbert polynomial $P$ ($\chi(O_{Z_s}(m))=P(m)$ for all $s\in S$), i.e. functor $\operatorname{Hilb}^P_X(S)=\{Z\subset X\times S \text{ closed, flat over } S, P_{Z_s}=P\}$ representable by $\operatorname{Hilb}^P$.
  In particular $\operatorname{Hilb}^n(X)=\operatorname{Hilb}^{P=n}(X)$ for 0-dimensional length-$n$ subschemes as $P(m)=n$, and $\operatorname{Hilb}_X = \coprod_P \operatorname{Hilb}^P$.
  Object $\operatorname{Hilb}^P(X) : \mathsf{Sch}$ with universal family $U\subset X\times\operatorname{Hilb}^P$ flat over $\operatorname{Hilb}$.
  Interface `X.hilbert_scheme(P) -> Hilb^P(X) : Sch` and `X.hilbert_scheme_of_points(n) -> Hilb^n(X)`, and for $C$ curve $\operatorname{Hilb}^n(C)=\operatorname{Sym}^n C$, for $S$ surface $\operatorname{Hilb}^n(S)$ smooth $2n$-dim with Hilbert-Chow $\operatorname{Hilb}^n(S)\to\operatorname{Sym}^n S$.

* Honest holonomy groups: for $E\to X$ vector bundle $E: \mathsf{Vect}(X)$ with connection $\nabla: E\to E\otimes\Omega^1_X$ (flat or not), $\operatorname{Hol}(\nabla)$ is not a formal `monodromy_group` string.
  It is the subgroup $\operatorname{Hol}_x(\nabla) \leq \mathrm{GL}(E_x)$ generated by parallel transport $P_\gamma: E_x\to E_x$ along loops $\gamma: [0,1]\to X$ piecewise smooth with $\gamma(0)=\gamma(1)=x$, where $P_\gamma$ is defined by ODE $\nabla_{\gamma'}s=0$.
  For flat $\nabla$, $P_\gamma$ depends only on $[\gamma]\in\pi_1(X,x)$ and $\operatorname{Hol}_x = \operatorname{Mon}_x$ is the monodromy image $\rho: \pi_1\to\mathrm{GL}(E_x)$ already intaken via $\mathsf{FlatConn} \to \mathsf{LocSys}$; for non-flat, $\operatorname{Hol}$ is the Ambrose-Singer group generated by curvature via $\operatorname{Hol}_x = \langle P_\gamma^{-1}\circ R(u,v)\circ P_\gamma\rangle$.
  For Riemannian $\nabla=\text{Levi-Civita}$ on $X : \mathsf{RiemMfd}$ with $T_X$, $\operatorname{Hol}_x \leq O(T_x)$ Berger list, and for $G_2$, $\mathrm{Spin}(7)$ etc. restricted holonomy.
  Interface `nabla.holonomy_group(x) -> Hol_x : Groups` as subgroup $\operatorname{Hol}_x \leq \mathrm{GL}_{\operatorname{rank} E}(k)$ presented via generators $P_{\gamma_i}$ for loops $\gamma_i$ generating $\pi_1$ (effective via validated ODE transport as in CAP monodromy lead), and restricted $\operatorname{Hol}^0_x$ identity component via null-homotopic loops.

Intended owners: `categories/moduli/character_varieties.py` (`CharacterVariety(pi,G)=Rep(pi,G)//G : Sch` with $\operatorname{Rep}(\pi,G) \subset G^S$, $M_B(X,G)=X(\pi_1(X),G)$ Betti moduli as $\operatorname{Spec}(k[\operatorname{Rep}]^G)$), `categories/moduli/git_quotients.py` (`GITQuotient(X,G,L)=X//_L G : Sch` via $\operatorname{Proj}(\bigoplus\Gamma(L^n)^G)$ with $\pi: X^{ss}\to X/\!/G$ and $X^s/G$), `categories/moduli/hilbert_schemes.py` (`HilbertScheme(X,P)=Hilb^P(X) : Sch` with `HilbertFunctor` representability, $\operatorname{Hilb}^n$ as $P=n$, universal $U$), `categories/connections/holonomy.py` (`HolonomyGroup(nabla,x) <= GL(E_x)` via $P_\gamma$ transport, $\operatorname{Hol}^0$, Berger/Ambrose-Singer).
Not a free `character_variety(pi,G)` or `holonomy(nabla)` returning matrices — `pi.character_variety(G)` and `nabla.holonomy_group(x)` on the group/bundle objects with $\operatorname{Rep}$ scheme $G^S$ and $G$-invariants behind $X/\!/G$ and $P_\gamma$ ODE.

## Desired capability: group (co)homology with Tate/transfers/traces/norms/induction, surface groups, group objects, Higgs/NAH, RR/index/Poincaré/Chern, 4-manifold Freedman, spinnability/Arf/KS/Â, equivariant, Hodge ops, cobordism/TQFT, normal bundles — note 2026-09-15

Need group homology/cohomology, Tate cohomology, transfers, traces, norms, induction/restriction and their co-versions.
Need surface groups $\pi_1\Sigma_g$ as explicitly finitely presented groups, algebraic groups, Lie groups, group schemes, Higgs bundles, local systems, flatness for vector bundles, (harmonic) metrics, semisimplicity and complete reducibility for representations, basic non-abelian Hodge theory, operationalize Riemann-Roch computations, various index theorems (e.g. Atiyah-Singer), Gauss-Bonnet.
Need to be able to compute full Poincaré series as OGFs (formal power series) and thus extra Betti numbers.
Need to operationalize calculus with Chern classes and Chern characters.
Ideally be able to get $H^*(X;ZZ)/\operatorname{tors}$ for at least 4-manifolds, as a graded group but ideally a ring/$ZZ$-algebra, and thus be able to check homeomorphism for 4-manifolds using Freedman's theorem and constructing the lattice on $H^2$.
Operationalize algorithms and theorems to decide when a smooth manifold is spinnable (e.g. by Rohlin, Kervaire–Milnor theorem, Freedman-Kirby).
Need the Arf invariant, Kirby–Siebenmann invariant, $\hat{A}$ genus.
Need enough to operationalize Hirzebruch–Riemann–Roch and Chern-Gauss-Bonnet.
Differential operators and the symbol map, and their index maps.
Todd class.
Need to be able to operational $G$-equivariant cohomology and $K$ theory.
Need exterior derivatives, Hodge Laplacians, Hodge star, etc. Need operationalizations of known cobordism rings, and in some cases the ability to detect if manifolds are cobordant.
Need cobordism categories in order to define TQFTs as functors.
Need normal bundles of immersions and embeddings.

* Group (co)homology is not $H^n(G,M)=\operatorname{Ext}^n_{Z[G]}(Z,M)$ as a bare `Ext` call without $G$-structure.
  For $G : \mathsf{Groups}$ (finite, finitely presented $G=\langle S\mid R\rangle$, Lie, profinite, group scheme), $M : ZZ[G]\text{-}\mathsf{Mod}$ ($G$-module as $\mathsf{Ab}$ with $G$-action $\rho: G\to\operatorname{Aut}(M)$), $H_n(G,M)=\operatorname{Tor}_n^{ZZ[G]}(ZZ,M)=H_n(M\otimes_{ZZ[G]} P_\bullet)$ and $H^n(G,M)=\operatorname{Ext}^n_{ZZ[G]}(ZZ,M)=H^n(\operatorname{Hom}_{ZZ[G]}(P_\bullet,M))$ where $P_\bullet\to ZZ$ is projective resolution of trivial $G$-module $ZZ$ (e.g. bar resolution $B_\bullet(G)=ZZ[G^{n+1}]$ as free $ZZ[G]$-module).
  Tate cohomology $\hat{H}^n(G,M)$ for finite $G$ extends to $n\in ZZ$ via complete resolution $\hat{P}_\bullet$ (norm $N=\sum_{g\in G}g: M_G\to M^G$), with $\hat{H}^0=M^G/NM$, $\hat{H}^{-1}=\ker N / I_G M$, $\hat{H}^n=H^n$ ($n\geq 1$), $\hat{J}_n=H_n$ ($n\geq 1$). Transfers $\operatorname{tr}^G_H: H^*(H,M)\to H^*(G,M)$ and co-transfer $\operatorname{cor}: H_*(H)\to H_*(G)$, traces $\operatorname{tr}: M\to M^G$, norms $N_G: M\to M^G$, induction $\operatorname{Ind}_H^G: H\text{-}\mathsf{Mod}\to G\text{-}\mathsf{Mod}$ $\operatorname{Ind}_H^G M = ZZ[G]\otimes_{ZZ[H]}M$ left adjoint to restriction $\operatorname{Res}_H^G$, and coinduction $\operatorname{CoInd}_H^G = \operatorname{Hom}_{ZZ[H]}(ZZ[G],M)$ right adjoint, with Shapiro $H^*(G,\operatorname{CoInd})=H^*(H,M)$ and $H_*(G,\operatorname{Ind})=H_*(H,M)$.
  Also co-versions $\operatorname{coInd}$ vs $\operatorname{coRes}$ via $\operatorname{Hom}$/$\otimes$ duality.
  Interface `G.group_cohomology(n,M)` $\to H^n$, `G.tate_cohomology(n,M)` $\to \hat{H}^n$, `H.transfer(G,M)` etc. on the group object, with $P_\bullet$ as $D(ZZ[G])$ object and $\otimes^L_{ZZ[G]}$ already intaken.

* Surface groups $\pi_1\Sigma_g$ as explicitly finitely presented groups: $\pi_1\Sigma_g = \langle a_1,b_1,\ldots,a_g,b_g \mid \prod_{i=1}^g [a_i,b_i]=1\rangle : \mathsf{FinitelyPresentedGroups}$ with $g: NN$, genus $g$, $\Sigma_g$ closed orientable surface $\operatorname{Chi}=2-2g$, $H_1=Z^{2g}$, $H_2=Z$.
  Object `SurfaceGroup(g) : FPGroups` with presentation as datum $F(S)\twoheadrightarrow\pi$ as framed group `Framed(F(S), rels)` with word $r=\prod[a_i,b_i] : F(S)$.
  Non-orientable $N_g$ variant $\langle c_1,\ldots,c_g\mid\prod c_i^2=1\rangle$ when needed.
  This is data-bearing `Framed` FP group already modeled via `Groups().framed` / $\mathsf{FrGroups}$, not a string presentation.

* Group objects taxonomy as distinct categories with forgetful functors, not one $\mathsf{Groups}$ type:

  - Algebraic groups $G : \mathsf{GrpSch}/k$ ($G : \mathsf{Sch}/k$ with $m:G\times G\to G$, $e:\operatorname{Spec} k\to G$, $\operatorname{inv}$), e.g. $\mathrm{GL}_n, \mathrm{SL}_n, \mathrm{SO}_n, \mathrm{Sp}_{2n}, \mathrm{PGL}_n, G_m, G_a, \text{elliptic}$.

  - Lie groups $G : \mathsf{LieGroups}$ smooth manifold with smooth $m$, e.g. $U(n), \mathrm{SU}(n), \mathrm{Sp}(n), \mathrm{SO}(n)$, with $\operatorname{Lie}(G)=T_eG$ and $\exp: \operatorname{Lie}(G)\to G$.

  - Group schemes over $ZZ$ / $\operatorname{Spec} k$ with base-change $G_R = G\times_{\operatorname{Spec} Z} \operatorname{Spec} R$; $G(k)$ $k$-points.
    Each has $\mathsf{AlgGrp}\to\mathsf{LieGrp}$ analytification $G^{an}$, $\mathsf{GrpSch}\to\mathsf{Groups}$ forgetful $G\mapsto G(k)$.

* Higgs bundles, local systems, flatness, metrics, NAH: for $X : \mathsf{SmProj}/C$ ($X$ smooth projective curve/surface/variety), $\text{Higgs bundle} = (E, \varphi)$ with $E : \mathsf{VecBun}(X)$ ($E : \mathsf{QCoh}$ locally free), $\varphi: E\to E\otimes\Omega^1_X$ $O_X$-linear ($\varphi\wedge\varphi=0$ integrability, as $\text{Higgs field}$), semistability via $\mu(F)<\mu(E)$ for $\varphi$-invariant $F\subset E$.
  $\text{Local system} = L : \mathsf{LocSys}(X^{an})$ as $\mathsf{FlatConn}^{rs}$ object $(E,\nabla)$ with $\nabla^2=0$ already intaken via $\mathsf{Conn}\to\mathsf{FlatConn}\to\mathsf{LocSys}$ and Riemann-Hilbert.
  Flatness for $E$ means existence of flat $\nabla$ (i.e. $E$ in $\mathsf{FlatConn}$ image under forgetful $\mathsf{FlatConn}\to\mathsf{VecBun}$; Atiyah class $\operatorname{at}(E)\in\operatorname{Ext}^1(E,E\otimes\Omega^1)=0$ iff flat when $X$ curve).
  Harmonic metric $h: E\to\bar E^\vee$ Hermitian with Hitchin equations $F_h + [\varphi, \varphi^{*h}]=0, \bar\partial_E \varphi=0$ (Kobayashi-Hitchin).
  Semisimplicity / complete reducibility for $\rho: \pi_1(X)\to\mathrm{GL}_n(C)$ = $\rho$ semisimple ($C[\pi]\text{-module}$ sum of simples) $\Leftrightarrow$ polystable Higgs $(E,\varphi)$ with $\deg=0$ via NAH. Basic non-abelian Hodge is the homeomorphism of moduli $M_{Dol}(X,r) \cong M_{dR}(X,r) \cong M_B(X,r)$ where $M_{Dol}=\mathrm{Higgs}^{ss}/\sim$, $M_{dR}=\mathrm{Flat}^{ss}/\sim$ (flat connections), $M_B=\operatorname{Rep}(\pi_1,r)/\!/\mathrm{GL}_r$ (Betti).
